`appaṭiyāṉāl nīrum umakkut terinta ōr iṉiya pāṭṭaip pāṭukiṟatutāṉē. avvāṟu pāṭiṉāl aḵtu umakku iṉṉum mikunta makiḻcciyait tarum. ētō ciṟitu pāṭum eṉṟu colli avarait toṭṭu acaittu viṭṭāl, uṭaṉē avar tamakkut terinta toru pāṭṭaip pāṭāniṟpar. ciṟitunēram pāṭiyapiṉ `pāṭṭu mika naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatu. pōtum, eṉṟu colli niṟuttiviṭuka. iṉṉum ivai pōṉṟa ettaṉaiyō vakaiyāṉa uruveḷit tōṟṟaṅkaḷai yellām avarpāl viḷaivikkalām. iṅṅaṉamellāñ ceytu pārttapiṉ, iraṇṭu uḷḷaṅkaikaḷaiyum mēṉmukamāka virittu nīṭṭi, avarait toṭṭō toṭāmalō muḻaṅkāliliruntu, talaivaraiyil mēlnōkkit taṭavi `nīr ippōtu viḻittuk koḷḷukiṟīr; kaṇṇait tiṟantu viḻittuk koḷḷum eṉṟu collumpōtē kaikaḷait taṭṭi avarai eḻuppiviṭuka.
iṉi, oṉṟaiviṭṭorunāḷō, kiḻamaik koru kālō, aṉṟit tiṅkaḷukkorukālō aṭuttaṭuttu varum nāṭpaṭṭa nōy uṭaiyārkku, niṉaivaṟṟa ittuyilai varuvittu, annōy varutaṟkuriya kālamuṟaiyai māṟṟi viṭṭāl annōy nīṅkiviṭutal tiṇṇam. eṇṇattaiyum narampaiyum paṟṟivarum nōykaḷait tīrttaṟku ivvaṟituyil mikavum ēṟṟatoṉṟākum. attakaiya nōy koṇṭārai ittuyilil neṭunēram irukkacceytal naṉṟu; orunāḷ muḻutum, allatu orukiḻamai muḻutuṅ kūṭa avarai ittūkkattil irukkac ceyyalām. narampiṉ taḷarcciyum valiviṉmaiyum mikutiyāyirukkuṅkāl, muṭimutal aṭikāṟum iraṇṭu kaikaḷālum kīḻnōkki muṉṉum piṉṉum taṭavuka; ataṉpiṉ nōyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilmaṭṭum taṭavuka. neñcap paiyiṉ acaivu valikuṟaintu kāṇappaṭumāṉāl, allatu aḵtu oḻuṅkiṉṟi naṭaipeṟumāṉāl avviṭattait taṭaviyapiṉ vāyiṉāl ūtuka. ataṉāl nōyāḷiyiṉ uṟuppukaḷukkuk katakatappum iṉiyatōr uṇarcciyun tōṉṟak kāṇalām. mikak koṭiya iraippirumalaiyum, tōṭpaṭṭaikaṭku naṭuvē piṭariyiliruntu mutukuneṭukak kīḻnōkki mella orē oḻuṅkāy ūtutalāl taṇiyac ceyyalām. āṉāl oṉṟu; nōyāḷi tamatu uṭampiṉmēṟ paṭṭuttuṇi aṇintiruppārāṉāl ataṉmēl vāyiṉāl ūtiṟ payaṉ uṇṭākātu; āṉatupaṟṟi avar pañcunūṟṟuṇi aṇiyumāṟu ceyvittu immuṟaiyaic ceyka. allatu tuṇiyai vilakkic ceyyattakkavarāyiruntāl avvāṟu ceytalum ām. iṅṅaṉam vāyiṉāl ūtuṅkāl maṟṟoṉṟum uṉṉittal vēṇṭum. neñciṉuḷ mūccuppai naṉku niṟaiyak kāṟṟai uḷḷiḻuttu, vāyai nōykoṇṭa iṭattiṉmēl neruṅka vaittu, uramākavum orē oḻuṅkākavum ūtit, tirumpavum mūccu iḻukkum poruṭṭu, mukattai appuṟamākat tiruppukaiyil, ūtiṉa iṭattiṉiṉṟum vetuvetuppu nīṅkāmaip poruṭṭu valatu kaiyai ataṉmēl vaittu ataṉaip pottik koḷka. mūcciḻukkuṅkāl ataṉaip pottiyum, tirumpa ūtuṅkāṟ kaiyai appāl eṭuttum iṅṅaṉamē palakāṟceyka.
iṉip, palvaliyāl varuntuvōrai niṉaivaṟṟa tuyiliṟ pōkacceytu, piṉvarumāṟu ceytāl anta nōyai uṭaṉē taṇiyac ceyyalām. palvali mukattil entap pakkattil irukkiṟatō anta iṭattiṉmēl valatu kaiyaic ciṟitunēram vaittal vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṟaku, valiyirukkum pakkamāyk kātiṉmēl melliyatoru tuṇṭuk kampaḷiyai maṭittup pōṭṭu, ataṉ mēl maṟupaṭiyuṅ kaiyai vaikka vēṇṭum; maṟṟakkaiyait talaimēl vaittu akkampaḷiyiṉ mēṉ muṉaiyaip piṭittukkoṇṭu, ataṉ ūṭē kātukkuḷ valuvāy ūtuka. annōy uṭaṉē nīṅkavēṇṭumeṉṉum muṉaippōṭu ivvāṟu mūṉṟu, nāṉku muṟai ceyka. vetuvetuppāṉa ōr uṇarcci valiyuḷḷa palliṉkaṇ uṇṭāka, uṭaṉē avvali taṇiyum. kaṭaiciyāka ūtiyapiṉ, kātiṉmēliṭṭa kampaḷiyai akaṟṟiviṭuka. immuṟaiyāl innōyai uṭaṉē pōkkalāmēṉum, itaṉai vērōṭu kaḷaital iḵtoṉṟāl maṭṭum muṭiyātu. malakkuṭaril aḻukkuc cērāmal nāṭōṟuṅ kaḻuviviṭutalālum, kālaimālai iraṇṭuvēḷai nīrai naṉṟāyk kotikka vaittup, palvaliyuḷḷa pakkattiṉ uḷḷum puṟampum av āviyiṟ paṭumpaṭi ceytu cevvaiyāy ataṉai viyarkka vaittalālum annōyai aṭiyōṭu tolaikkalām.
iṉik koṭumaiyāṉa talaivali koṇṭavarkaḷukkum ataṉai uṭaṉē nīṅkac ceyyalām. nōyāḷikkup piṉṉē niṉṟukoṇṭu, iraṇṭu kaikaḷaiyum nōyāḷiyiṉ neṟṟi mēṟ ciṟitunēram vaittu, aṅkiruntu kaṉṉappoṭṭukaḷaic ciṟitu aḻuttiyapaṭiyāy mellap piṉṉukkut taṭavip, piṟaku kaikaḷai uccantalaikkuk koṇṭupōy, aṅkiruntu avaṟṟai eṭuttu utaṟuka. tirumpavum iṅṅaṉamē ceyka. ippaṭiyāka aintumutaṟ patiṉaintu nimiṭaṅkaḷ ceytāl, talaivaliyiṉ koṭumai taṇiyum. piṉṉum atu ciṟitiruntāl, valinīkkun tailam iṭṭuc ciṟitu tēyttāl atu muṟṟum nīṅkum. maṟupaṭiyun talainōy varātapaṭi malakkuṭarait tuppuravu ceytu, tīṉippai cevvaiyākum poruṭṭuc cūṭāṉa nalla vennīr aruntuka.
iṉip poruttup piṭippu mutalāṉa nōyāl mika varuntuvōkkum immuṟaiyil attuṉpam eḷitil nīṅkum. nōy koṇṭa poruttiṉmēṟ, kaṭaiviralai akaṟṟi maṟṟai viralkaḷaic cērttu nērokkappiṭittu, aṅkiruntu kīḻnōkkit taṭavik kīḻē avvuṟuppiṉ muṉaikku vantatum iraṇṭu kaikaḷaiyum utaṟiviṭṭuk kaikaḷaip pakkaccāyvāy mēluyartti, maṟupaṭiyum aṅkiruntu avvāṟē taṭavi utaṟuka. iṅṅaṉam palamuṟai ceytāl avvali irunta iṭattai viṭṭuk kīḻ iṟaṅkum; appuṟam ataṉai aṟavē nīkkutal eḷitu. immuṟaiyōṭu, poruttukkaḷil uḷḷa piṭippai iḷakac ceytaṟkuriya tailaṅkaḷ iṭṭut tēyttalum, aṅṅaṉan tēyttaṟkumuṉ piṭippuḷḷa iṭattai nīrāviyiṟ kāṭṭutalum peritum naṉmai payakkum.
iṅṅaṉamē veppunōy, kātuvali, kaṇvali, nīrkkōvai, narampuvali mutalāṉavaikaḷaiyum niṉaivaṟṟa tuyiliṉ utaviyāl mēṟkūṟiya muṟaippaṭi ceytu eḷitiṟ pōkkalām. immuṟaikaḷai oruvar evvaḷavukkuc ceytu ceytu paḻakukiṉṟārō, avvaḷavukku avar annōykaḷai yellām oru noṭippoḻutil nīkkavallunar āvar. immuṟaikaḷai vaḻuvāmaṟ paḻakum paḻakkamē innōykaḷait tīrttaṟkuc ciṟanta maruntākum. immuṟaikaḷai naṉku paḻakit tērcci peṟṟa oruvar (Dr. Herbert. A. Parkin, M.D. See New Thought Dec No. 1903.) maruntukaḷiṟ ṟīrāta cuvācakācam eṉṉum īḷai nōyāl varuntiṉa orumātarārkku ataṉait tīrtta uṇmai varalāṟṟiṉai iṅkē moḻipeyart teḻutukiṉṟām:-
orunāḷ mālaik kālattil, muppattiraṇṭu āṇṭuḷḷa oru mātaraic ceṉṟupārkkumpaṭi aḻaikkappaṭṭēṉ. ammātar patiṉaintu āṇṭukaḷāka nirampavuṅ koṭumaiyāṉa cuvācakācattāl nirampat tuṉpuṟṟu vantaṉar. mutaṉmutal avvammaiyārkku annōy vantapōtu ataṉai ārāyntu pārttavar aḵtu elumpurukki nōy eṉṟaṉar. piṉṉarp perukkak kaṇṇāṭi (Microscope) vaḻakkattukku vanta cila āṇṭukaḷil annōyai ārāyntu kāṇa atu cuvācakācam eṉṉum īḷai nōyāmeṉṟu uṟutippaṭuttiṉar. yāṉ anta ammaiyaip pārkkac ceṉṟapōtu, avar teṟkēyuḷḷa ōr ūriliruntu appōtutāṉ aṅkē vantucērntaṉar; avvūriṟ pallāṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟu taṅkiyum avarkku ētum naṉmai viḷaintilatu. paṭukkaiyiṟ pala talaiyaṇaikaḷai aṭukki avaṟṟiṉmēṟ cāynta paṭiyāy iḷaippiṉāl tuṉpuṟṟuk koṇṭu avar uṭkārntirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. avartam vāyitaḻkaḷ nīlaniṟamāyum kaikālkaḷ cilleṉṟum iruntaṉa. palamaṇi nēraṅkaḷāy nōy nirampa mummuramāka iruntatu; aṉṟaik kālaimutal avvammai ētōr uṇavum eṭukkavillai. eṉṉiṭam avar kucukucuveṉṟu pēcutaṟkumēṟ pēca iyalavillai; appaṭip pēcuvatum mikka varuttamākavē iruntatu; ēṉeṉil avar muṟṟuṅ kaḷaippaṭaintiruntār.
`yāṉ appaṭukkaiyiṉ pakkattē amarntu, avvammaiyār kaikaḷil oṉṟai eṉ kaiyiṟ ṟūkki vaittuk koṇṭu `ippōtu nīṅkaḷ paṭuntuṉpattai nīkkum poruṭṭu yāṉ iṅku varuvikkappaṭṭēṉ; ciṟitu nērattil nīṅkaḷ mūccut taṭaiyiṉṟi viṭuvīrkaḷeṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ. ippōtu uṅkaḷiṭam yāṉ ētoṉṟuṅ kēḷēṉ; nāṉ uṅkaḷai nōkkic colvaṉavaṟṟil uṅkaḷ karuttaic ciṟitu nēram patiyavaikka vēṇṭuvatutāṉ eṉakku vēṇṭum. uṅkaḷ kaṇkaḷai mūṭikkoḷḷuṅkaḷ, ciṟitu nērattil ellām ōyvāyirukkum ōr uṇarcci uṅkaḷ uṭampeṅkum varak kāṇpīrkaḷ; mūccu ōṭṭamum varavara nīṇṭu āḻntu cellum. uṅkaḷ mārpuk kūṭṭiṉ mēlellām yāṉ kaiyāl tēykkap pōkiṉṟēṉ; appaṭic ceykaiyil mūcciṉ ōṭṭam varavarat taṭaiyiṉṟic cellum, eṉṟu colliyapaṭiyē mārpaiyum nuraiyīralukku nērēyuḷḷa mutukaiyum valuvāyt tēykkat tuvaṅkiṉēṉ; atē nērattil, nuraiyīral varavarat taṭai tīrkiṉṟateṉavum, mūccōṭṭam āḻamāy nīṇṭu celkiṉṟa teṉavum, narampu valiyum accamuṅ kavalaiyum akalukiṉṟaṉa veṉavum, varavarat tūkka mayakkamum uṇṭākiṉṟateṉavum, avarukku vēṇṭuvaṉa nalla uṇavun tūkkamumē yeṉavum iṉṉuñ ciṟitu nērattil ciṟitu cāṟu parukat takkavaḷavu mūccōṭṭam taṭaiyiṉṟi nikaḻum eṉavum, ataṉ piṟṟūkkam varumeṉavum kaṭṭurai kūṟikkoṇṭē vantēṉ.
kuṟippiṭṭa inta muṟaiyākap pattu nimiṭaṅkaḷ varaiyil tēytta piṟakuṅ, kaṭṭurai kūṟiya piṟakum nōy koṇṭa avvammaiyār mika eḷitāy mūccu viṭutalaiyum iratta ōṭṭam cīrppaṭṭuc cellutalaiyuṅ kaṇṭu uvappuṟṟēṉ. ataṉpiṉ, mūccōṭṭam eḷitāy naṭaipeṟa naṭaipeṟac cāyntu koḷḷa vaittirukkun talaiyaṇaikaḷai oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāy eṭuttukkoṇṭē vantu kaṭaiciyāka avaraip paṭukkai maṭṭattiṟ kiṭattuvōm eṉṟuṅ kaṭṭuraittēṉ. mutal talaiyaṇaiyai eṭuttuviṭṭavuṭaṉ avvammai paṭippaṭiyāy ataṟku aṭutta talaiyaṇaiyiṟ cāyalāṉār. annēramellām, `taṭaiyillā mūccōṭṭat taip paṟṟiyum, `tūkkattaip paṟṟiyum, `paciyaip paṟṟiyuṅ kaṭṭuraikaḷait toṭarpākac colli vantēṉ. avvammaiyār iraṇṭān talaiyaṇaimēṟ cevvaiyāyc cāyntu koṇṭirukkaiyil naṉṟāyt tāḷitta cūṭāṉa cāṟu ciṟitu avar aruntak koṭuttōm. piṉṉuñ ciṟutunērañ ceṉṟapiṉ, maṟṟat talaiyaṇaikaḷaiyum eṭukkalāṉēṉ; iṅṅaṉamāka irupatu nimiṭaṅkaḷil ellāt talaiyaṇaikaḷaiyum eṭuttuviṭṭu, ayarntu uṟaṅkum avvammaiyaip paṭukkai maṭṭattil kiṭakkaviṭṭōm. avar iraṇṭumaṇinēran tūṅkiṉār. ataṉpiṉ iṉṉuñ ciṟitu uṇavu koṭukkap paṭṭatu. tirumpavum avar tūkkaṅkoṇṭu paṭuttār. aṭutta irupatumaṇinērattil iraṇṭumaṇi nērattiṟku oru muṟaiyāka avarukku uṇavu tarappaṭṭatu.
malaccikkal, ceriyāmai, tūkkamiṉmai, paciyiṉmai mutalāṉavai nīṅkum poruṭṭuk kaṭṭurai moḻikaḷ kūṟiya paṭiyāy, ovvoru nāḷuṅ kālai mālai iraṇṭu poḻutukaḷāka iraṇṭu kiḻamaikaḷ varaiyil avvammai yārukku immuṟaiyaic ceytu vantēṉ. uṭaṉē avar nalampeṟalāṉār. mutaṟṟaram immuṟaiyaic ceyta vaḷavāṉē avvīḷai nōy tirumpa varum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇap paṭavillai. avaruṭampiṉ ovvoru nikaḻcciyun tiruttappaṭṭatu. iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷil ciṟitēṟakkuṟaiya ēḻuvīcai eṭai mikutiyākap peṟṟār. avvīḷai nōy avarai viṭṭakaṉṟu mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. avartam vāḻnāḷellām varuntik koṇṭirunta pala vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa nōykaḷai akalac ceytamaiyē avvammaiyār annōy muṟṟuntīrap peṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu yāṉ karutukiṉṟēṉ.
mikak koṭiya kācanōyait tīrtta amerikka maruttuvariṉ ivvuṇmai varalāṟu koṇṭu, maruntukaḷiṉ utavi vēṇṭāmalē ettakaiya nōyaiyum ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟaiyāl tīrttu viṭalāmeṉpatu iṉitu viḷaṅku kiṉṟataṉṟō?
15. taṇṇīr mantirittuk koṭuttal
iṉi, uḷḷē parukutaṟkāvatu, veḷiyē uṭampiṟ ṟeḷittuk koḷḷutaṟkāvatu, allatu talaimuḻukutaṟkāvatu taṇṇīr mantirittuk koṭukkum vaḻakkam nammaṉōruḷḷuṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ayalnāṭukaḷiluḷḷa veṇmakkaḷ immuṟai nōy nīkkutaṟkup peritum utavutalai naṉku ārāyntu pārttu itaṉaiyuṅ kaikkoṇṭu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. aṟituyil muṟaikaḷiṟ paḻakit tērcci peṟṟa oruvarkku niṉaivāṟṟal nirampa valivēṟi niṟkumātalāl, an niṉaivāṟṟalāl untappaṭum avaratuṭampiluḷḷa miṉcattu avartaṅ kaṇkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavum coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavum viralnuṉikaḷiṉ vaḻiyākavum puṟattē ōṭip puṟattuḷḷa poruḷkaḷaiyum uyirkaḷaiyum iyaṅkumāṟum iyaṅkāmal vaṟitē irukkumāṟum ceytu viṭukiṉṟatu. innāvalantīvilum niṉaivāṟṟal mikuntuḷḷōr palaruḷar.
avar tammai vaḻipaṭṭārai vāḻavum, tammaip puṟampaḻittāraic cāvavuñ ceyta uṇmai varalāṟukaḷ āṅkāṅkuḷḷārkkut teriyāmal irā. toḻu nōy koṇṭum, īḷai mutaliya koṭunōykaḷāl varuntiyum vanta cilar attakaiya periyārai etirppaṭṭu avar tan tirunōkkan tammēṟ paṭṭa aḷavāṉē annōy tīrappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. vēṟucilar avaraip paḻittut tītu ceytamaiyāṉē avarāl vaiyappaṭṭut tāmum taṅkuṭumpamum vērōṭu aḻintu pōyiṉar. nīrāvi vaṇṭiyiṟ ceṉṟa attakaiya periyārai aṟiyāmal avaraik kīḻiṟakki viṭṭamaiyāṉē, avar avvaṇṭi ōṭāmaiyil niṟkacceyta nikaḻccikaḷum piṉṉar avarai vēṇṭi irantamaiyāṉē avar iraṅki avaṟṟai ōṭacceyta nikaḻccikaḷum innāṭṭiṉ kaṇuḷḷār palar nērē kaṇṭumiruppar. veṟikoṇṭu evarkkum etaṟkum aṭaṅkāmal ōṭit tirintu uḻakkiya kaḷiṟṟiyāṉaikaḷaiyuṅ kutiraikaḷ āṉēṟukaḷ naccuppāmpukaḷ varippulikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum tamatu kaṭpārvaiyālum oru collālum aṭakkiya periyōraiyum palar pārttiruppar. amerikkāvil immuṟaikaḷil naṉku tērcci peṟṟa oruvar oru maṇikku nūṟṟirupatu mail vaḻiyōṭum oru nīrāvi vaṇṭiyai oru noṭiyil ōṭāmal niṟutti viṭṭaṉareṉṟu aṅkiruntuvarum veḷiyīṭukaḷiṟ paṭittuṇarn tirukkiṉṟōm. iṅṅaṉamellāṅ kaṇkūṭāka aṟiyappaṭum uṇmai nikaḻccikaḷiliruntu, niṉaivāṟṟal mikap peṟṟōrāl untappaṭum miṉṉāṟṟal avartaṅ kaṇkaḷ coṟkaḷ viral nuṉikaḷ eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ vaḻicceṉṟu arumperum putumaikaḷai viḷaikka vallatātalai iṉituṇarkiṉṟēm allamō?
ivvāṟu untappaṭum miṉ āṟṟalai nōyāḷiyiṉ uṭampiṟ celuttiṉāl aḵtu ettuṇai naṉmaiyai evvaḷavu viraiviṟ ceyyumeṉpatai nām eṭuttuc collutalum vēṇṭumō? nōy tīrkkum niṉaiviṉ muṉaippiṉāl untappaṭum miṉcattait taṉṉiṭattu vāṅki ataṉai nōyāḷiyiṉ uṭampiṟ celuttutaṟku iṭainiṉṟu utavum poruḷkaḷuḷ taṇṇīrē mikac ciṟantatāyirukkiṉṟatu. ēṉeṉṟāl, namatuṭampil mukkāṟpaṅku nīrpporuḷāṉa irattattāl niṟaikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu; avvirattamē miṉcattait taṉkaṇ ēṟṟu uṭampiṉuḷ eṅkum ōṭi ataṉai vaḷarttu varukiṉṟatu; nuṇṇiya miṉ cattōṭu kalantu ulāvutaṟku atupōl ōṭavalla nekiḻccipporuḷ oṉṟē payaṉpaṭuvatallatu, ēṉaik kaṭṭip poruḷkaḷ attuṇaip payaṉpaṭā. ātalāl, tūyataṇṇīril miṉcattai ēṟṟi ataṉai nōyāḷi parukutaṟkuk koṭuttalē naṉṟu. tūya kuḷirnta ūṟṟut taṇṇīr kiṇaṟṟut taṇṇīr kiṭaiyātapōtu, paṉaivellattai mantirittuc ciṟu māttiraikaḷākki oru ciṟu puṭṭilil aṭaittu vaittuk koṇṭu, avaṟṟaiyum nōyāḷikkuk koṭuttunōy tīrkkalām. allatu, uṇṇum uṇavaiyum, uṭukkum uṭaiyaiyuṅkūṭa mantirittuk koṭukkalām.
iṉit, taṇṇīril nōy tīrkkum miṉcattai ēṟṟumāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum; nallatōr ūṟṟilēṉuṅ kiṇaṟṟilēṉum tūya maṭpāṇṭattil mukanteṭuttuvanta tūya kuḷirnta nīrai oru maṭkuṭuvaiyiṟ peytu, ataṉai iṭatu kaiyil vaittukkoḷka. piṉpu, oḷivaṭivākiya iṟaivaṉuruvai niṉaintu uḷḷattai avvoḷivaṭiviṟ porutti, avvoḷi numatu uḷḷattiṟ pāyntu uṭampeṅkum niṟaintu niṟpatāka naṉṟāy uṟutti niṉaikka. aṅṅaṉañ ciṟitu nēram niṉaintapiṉ, valatu kaiyiṉ kaṭaic ciṟuviral oḻiya ēṉaiviralkaḷai oṉṟaṉmēloṉṟu paṭāmaṟ kuvittu, iṭatu kaiyil uḷḷa kuṭuvait taṇṇīrkkumēṟ piṭikka; kaṇkaḷaiyum imaiyāmal niṟutti, avvāṟu kuvitta viral nuṉikaḷil avaṟṟiṉ pārvaiyaiyum niṟuttuka; ataṉpiṉ,uṭampil niṟainta avvoḷi nummuṭaiya kaṇkaḷiṉ vaḻiyāyuṅ kuvitta viral nuṉikaḷiṉ vaḻiyāyum ōṭi akkuṭuvait taṇṇīriṟ pāyvatāka uṟutti niṉaikka; aṅṅaṉam niṉaikkaiyilēyē avviralkaḷai naṭuṅkuvatu pōl acaikka; ataṉpiṉ `nōy nīkkum miṉcattāl niṟainta ittaṇṇīr innōyāḷiyiṉ innōyai uṭaṉē nīkkuka! eṉṟum aṭuttaṭuttu uḷḷē collik koḷka. ivvāṟu ceytāṉapiṉ, akkuṭuvait taṇṇīrai orumaṇi nērattiṟku orukāl oru vāykoṇṭa vaḷavu parukak koṭuttu varuka.
akkuṭuvait taṇṇīrai mēṉmūṭikoṇṭu iṟuka mūṭivaikka vēṇṭum. uḷḷukkup parukak koṭuttalēyaṉṟi, orukuṭan taṇṇīrai iṅṅaṉamē mantirittuk koṭuttu nōyāḷi ataṉait talaiyil viṭṭu muḻukavuñ ceyyalām. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷuṅ kaṇṇāṭi ēṉaṅkaḷuṅ kaḷimpu turu mutaliya naccupporuḷ illātaṉa vākaiyāl, ivaikaḷē immuṟaikkup payaṉpaṭuttat takkavai. celvamuḷḷavarkaḷ poṉ veḷḷikaḷāṟ ceyta ēṉaṅkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttalām. ivvāṟu niṉaivu muṉaippāl miṉcattu ēṟṟappaṭṭa taṇṇīr potuvāṉa taṇṇīriṉum vēṟupāṭuṭaiya teṉpataṟku, miṉcattu ēṟṟiya taṇṇīraiyum aḵtēṟṟāta taṇṇīraiyum vēṟṟumai teriviyāmalē nuṇṇuṇarvu mikka oru ciṟumi kaiyiṟ koṭuttuc cuvaittup pārkkumpaṭi ceytāl, avaḷ avviraṇṭaṟkumuḷḷa cuvai vēṟupāṭṭai naṉkaṟintu uraippaḷ.
iṉṉum, iṭatu kaiyāl miṉ cattu ēṟṟappeṟṟa taṇṇīrkkum, valatu kaiyāl miṉcattu ēṟṟappeṟṟa taṇṇīrkkuṅkūṭa vēṟupāṭu peritu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. im muṟaikaḷil naṉṟāyp paḻakit tērccipeṟṟa oruvar tam iṭatu kaiyai oru kiṇṇan taṇṇīriṉmēṟ kaviḻttup piṭitta paṭiyāyt tamatu miṉṉāṟṟal atiṟ pāyvatāka ōr aintu nimiṭa nēram niṉaittirukka; piṉṉar vēṟoru kiṇṇan taṇṇīriṉ mēl aṅṅaṉamē tamatu valatu kaiyaip piṭittapaṭiyāy avvaḷavu nēramē niṉaittirukka. piṟaku, nuṇṇuṇarvu mikka oru ciṟumi kaiyiṟ koṭuttu avviraṇṭaiyum oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāyc cuvaittu avaṟṟiṟkuḷḷa cuvai vēṟupāṭṭait terivikkumpaṭi kaṟpittāl, iṭatu kaivaḻiyē miṉcattēṟiya taṇṇīr vetuvetuppākavum veṟuppāṉa cuvaiyuṭaiya tākavum irukkiṉṟateṉavum, valatukai vaḻiyē aḵtēṟiya taṇṇīr iṉitākavum paḷapaḷappākavum irukkiṉṟateṉavum avaḷ kūṟuvaḷ. iṉṉum pala kiṇṇan taṇṇīrkaḷukku iṭaiyē iṅṅaṉam miṉcattēṟṟiya kiṇṇam oṉṟaṉaiyuṅ kalantu vaittu, avai tammai ovvoṉṟāyc cuvaittup pārttu, avaṟṟiṉ cuvai vēṟupāṭu terivikkumpaṭi nōyāḷi oruvarkku moḻintāl, avar miṉcattēṟiya nīriṉcuvai maṟṟaiyavaṟṟiṉ vēṟāyiruttalai naṉkuṇarntu ataṉait terintu cuṭṭuvar.
iṉi, nīril miṉcattu ēṟṟikkoṭuttu nōytīrkkum muṟaiyaip paḻakavēṇṭupavar nōyaṟṟa tūya vuṭampum ellārkkum naṉmai ceyya virumpun tūya vuḷḷamuṅ kaṭavuḷiṭattuk kaṉinta aṉpum uṭaiyavarāy iruttal iṉṟiyamaiyāta tākum.
attakaiyōr iḷaippuk kaḷaippiṉṟik kiḷarcci yōṭu irukkuṅkālaṅ kālaippoḻutē yākaiyāl, annēramē im muṟaiyāl nōy tīrttaṟku ēṟṟa kālamām. mikavum ayarcci yaṭaiyattakka uḻaippiṉai nāḷ muḻutuñ ceyyāmaliruntāl, ellām amaitiyuṟṟirukkum mālaik kālamum itaṉaic ceytaṟku ēṟṟatēyākum. nōyāḷi amaiti koṇṭirukkuṅ kālamum itaṟku icaintateṉṟuṇartal vēṇṭum. atu niṟka.
16. tīyapaḻakkaṅkaḷai oḻikkummuṟai
iṉi, mēṟpiṟavikaḷil iṭaiyaṟātu paḻakiya paḻakkattāṟ palar piṟakkumpōtē appaḻakkattiṉ payaṉākac cila tīya taṉmaikaḷai yuṭaiyarāyp piṟantu, vaḷarntu, nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟa, attaṉmaikaḷukku otta tīya ceyalkaḷaic ceytu, tammaiyun tuṉpuṟuttip piṟaraiyun tuṉpuṟuttip piṟavippayaṉai vīṇākki māyntu pōkiṉṟaṉar. ivar piṟakkumpōtē ittakaiya tīya iyaṟkaikaḷai uṭaṉ koṇṭuvantu, piṉṉar avai vērūṉṟi vaḷartaṟkēṟṟa tīyōr cērkkaiyāl uraṅkoṇṭu muṟṟa, ataṉpiṉ evarāṉum māṟṟutaṟku ēlāta koṭiyōrāy ulakiṟkup pala perun tīṅkukaḷai iḻaittu māḷkiṉṟaṉar. ittaṉmaiyōrait tiruttutaṟku aracaṉum avaṉāl ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa ciṟaikkaḷam mutalāṉa karuvikaḷum paṇṭu toṭṭu ulakiṉkaṇ mikuntiruntum, ivarait tirutti nalvaḻippaṭuttuvatu muṭintapāṭāyillai. caṭṭattukku māṟākavē tuṇintu naṭappōr palarākac, caṭṭattiṉ piṭikku akappaṭāta tīneṟikaḷil oḷintum oḷiyāmalum naṭappōr palappalar. uḷḷaṭaṅki eriyun tīyai oru pakkattil mūṭiṉāl, atu maṟṟoru pakkattil erintu veḷippaṭutal pōlavum, nilattiṉuḷḷē vēriṉ uram nirampa uḷḷa oru muṇmarattai mēlē evvaḷavutāṉ veṭṭiṉālum atu mēlum mēlum taḷirttal pōlavum, makkaḷ uḷḷattē paṭintu mēṉmēl valuvāy vaḷarum pollāṅkaic cuvaṭaṟat tuṭaittaṟku icainta vaḻit tuṟaikaḷait tēṭāmaṟ, puṟattē evvaḷavu koṭumaiyāṉa caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷaic ceytu vaittālum, avaṟṟāl, tīya iyalpuḷḷōr tiruntuvar eṉṟu kaṉavilum niṉaittal vēṇṭām. akattē ūṟiya nañcai, akattē ceṉṟu uriñcum maruntē mikkavallatākum. atupōṟ, puṟattuc cellum uṇarvukaḷai maṭakki, akattucceṉṟu putaiyum `aṟituyiṟ kaṭṭuraikaḷ maṭṭumē, oruvar piṟaviyilēyē tammuṭaṉ koṇṭuvanta tīya iyaṟkaikaḷai māṟṟa vallatākumallāmal, vēṟupuṟattu muṟaikaḷ evaiyum avaṟṟai māṟṟa māṭṭāveṉṟu tiṇṇamāy uṇarntukoḷka. ciṟupiḷḷaikaḷā yirukkaiyilēyē ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟaikaḷāl makkaḷaic cīrtiruttutal eḷitu. avarkaḷ vaḷarntu periyarāṉapiṉ avarkaḷait tiruttutal aritu. ēṉeṉṟāl, avarkaḷ tammai aṟituyiliṟ celuttuvōrpāl aṇukavum māṭṭār, avar coṟkaḷukku iṇaṅkavum māṭṭār, āṉatupaṟṟip, periyōrkku aṭaṅki naṭattaṟkuriya piḷḷaimaip paruvattilēyē im muṟaikaḷiṉutaviyāl avarkaḷai nalvaḻippaṭuttutaṟku. peṟṟōrkaḷ aṉaivarum muyalvārkaḷāyiṉ ulakattil tīmaikaḷ aṟṟuppōm. ellārum nallavarkaḷāyt tattam āṟṟalukkēṟṟa pukaḻ puṇṇiyaṅkaḷaic ceytu nīṇṭa kālam immaiyilum iṉpavāḻkkaiyil vāḻntu, maṟumaiyilum aḻiyā vīṭṭiṉpattaip peṟuvar.
tāy tantaiyarkkum uvāttiyāyarkkuṅ kīḻppaṭiyāmal naṭantu kalvi payilāmaṟ kuṟumpaṉāyt tiriyum oru paiyaṉait tiruttuvatu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṟ kāṭṭutum: mēlē kāṭṭiya `aṟituyiliṟ celuttum muṟaikaḷil oṉṟāl avaṉait tūṅkac ceytu, avaṉai nōkkip piṉvarumāṟu colluka: ippōtu yāṉ collukīṟapaṭi yellām nī kīḻppaṭintu naṭakkakkaṭavāy. uṉṉai nāṉ ittūkkattiṉiṉṟum eḻuppiya piṟaku nī uṉ peṟṟōrkkum uvāttiyāyarkkuṅ kīḻppaṭintu naṭakkakkaṭavāy. uṉakkuk koṭukkappaṭum pāṭaṅkaḷai nī karuttāyp paṭittu avaṟṟil tērcciyaṭaiyak kaṭavāy. cuṟucuṟuppāy viḷaiyāṭa vēṇṭum nērattil viḷaiyāṭik karuttāyp paṭikka vēṇṭum nērattiṟ paṭittu oḻuṅkāka naṭakkak kaṭavāy. nī kaṟpavaikaḷai yellāñ cevvaiyāka niṉaivil vaikkakkaṭavāy. ellārkkum iṉiya nalla paiyaṉāynaṭantu mēṉmēṟ ceḻippuṭaṉ vaḷarak kaṭavāy. keṭṭa piḷḷaikaḷōṭuṅ keṭṭa peṇkaḷōṭum nī kalantu paḻakalākātu. yāṉ uṉṉai eḻuppiyapiṉ, yāṉ kūṟiya ivaikaḷai yellām niṉaivukūra māṭṭāy; āyiṉum; ippōtu yāṉ colliyapaṭiyē nī eppōtuma naṭakkak kaṭavāy. eṉṟu ikkaṭṭuraikaḷait tiruppit tiruppi mella aḻuttamāyt tīrmāṉamāṉa kuraliṟ palakāṟ colluka; orukāṟ colliyapiṉ ciṟitu nēram cummā iruntu piṟaku maṟṟorukāṟ colluka. ēṉeṉṟāl, aṟituyilil nuṇṇiya vuṭampiṉkaṇ niṟkum appaiyaṉuṭaiya aṟivil aṅṅaṉañ colliyavai naṉṟāyc ceṉṟu patitaṟkuc ciṟitu nēram vēṇṭum. immuṟai vaḻuvāmaṟ kālai mālai iraṇṭu vēḷaiyum oru kiḻamai  varaiyil avaṉai aṟituyiliṟ celuttik kaṭṭurai kūṟi eḻuppi vantāl, avaṉ tīya taṉmaikaḷellām māṟi muṟṟum nalla paiyaṉāyk kalviyilum naṭakkaiyilum maṟṟa ellā nalvakaikaḷilum mēmpaṭuvāṉ.
iṅṅaṉamē, pukaiyilaic curuṭṭup piṭippōṉaiyum, kaḷ, cārāyaṅ kuṭippōṉaiyum, mūkkuppoṭi yiṭuvōṉaiyum, iṉṉum poy cūtu kaḷavu uyirkkolai mutalāka ākātaṉa pala ceyvōṉaiyum mēṟcolliya paṭiyākavē aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytu, at tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷai viṭum paṭiyākak kaṭṭurai kūṟit, tirutti, nalvaḻippaṭuttalām.
iṉi, nōykaḷiṟ cērāta oru kuṟaipāṭu cilariṭaṅ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu `teṟṟuvāy eṉpatēyām. oru collaic coṉṉapiṉ ataṟkaṭutta collait toṭarntu collutaṟku iṭaṅkoṭāmal vāy mikat teṟṟutaliṉ, akkuṟaipāṭuṭaiyōr ataṉāl mikat tuṉpuṟukiṉṟaṉar. ākaiyāl, ataṉai nīkkutaṟkuriya oru muṟaiyai maṭṭum iṅkē viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟōm.
teṟṟit teṟṟip pēcun tikkuvāy uṭaiya oruvarai varuvittu, avarai āḻnta tūkkattiṟ pōkacceyka. ataṉpiṉ avarainōkki `nīr ellārum pēcuvataip pōṟ cevvaiyāyp pēcuvīr; umakku atil ētōr iṭaiñcalum irātu; taṅkutaṭai illāmal umakkup pēcavarum; eppōtum naṉṟāyp pēcuvīr, naṉṟāyp paṭippīr; coṟkaḷait toṭarpāka eḷitākac colluvīr, paṭikkum pōtum ovvoṉṟaiyun toṭarpāka naṉṟāyp paṭippīr eṉṟu palamuṟai tiruppit tiruppi āḻnta aḻuttamāṉa kuralil viraiyāmal metuvāyc colluka. iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭṭurai kūṟiyapiṉ, avarai avvaṟituyilil vaittukkoṇṭē, avartaṅ kaṇkaḷaimaṭṭun tiṟakkum paṭi kaṟpikka; uṭaṉē avartaṅ kaṇkaḷait tiṟappar; avar paṭikkattakka oru puttakattai avaratu kaiyiṟ koṭuttu atil oru pakutiyaip paṭikkumpaṭi kaṟpikka; aṟituyilil iruntapaṭiyākavē avar ataṉaip paṭikkaiyil vāytikkāmaṟ paṭippar. evvaḷavu tikkip pēcukiṟavarum aṟituyilil irukkaiyil teṟṟup paṭāmaṟ paṭippatai appōtu kāṇalām. piṟaku avar tam kaṇkaḷai mūṭikkoḷḷumpaṭi ceytu, iṅṅaṉamē avar eppōtun tikkāmaṟ cevvaiyāyp paṭippareṉa nālaintu muṟai tiruppit tiruppic collip, piṉṉum ōr aintu allatu pattu nimiṭam varaiyil avarai amaitiyāyt tūṅka viṭṭu, appuṟam avarai eḻuppi viṭuka.
iṉic, ciṟuvarkaḷaiyē yaṉṟi āṇṭil mutirnta āṭavaraiyum makaḷiraiyuṅkūṭa, mēṟkāṭṭiyavāṟē aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytu nām vēṇṭiyapaṭiyellām naṭakkumpaṭi kaṟpikkalām. ivvāṟellāñ ceytu payaṉpeṟutaṟku niṉaivōṭu kūṭiya aṟituyil ākātu; niṉaivu aṟṟa āḻnta aṟituyilē ivaikaḷaic ceytaṟku ēṟṟatoṉṟātalāl, ataṉai varuvitta piṟakē ikkaṭṭuraikaḷaic collutaṟku muyalal vēṇṭum. it tuyiliṉiṉṟu eḻuppappaṭṭapiṉ, tuyiṟṟuvōr colliyavaikaḷai viḻippu nilaiyil teriyāmalirukkumpaṭikkum, āṉālum avar appōtu colliyapaṭiyē eppōtum naṭakkumpaṭikkum vaṟpuṟuttum uraittal iṉṟiyamai yātatākum.
aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, aṟituyilukkuc cellamāṭṭāta allatu aṟituyiliṟcella viruppamillāta nōyāḷikaḷai allatu maṟṟavarkaḷai ataṉkaṭ celuttumāṟu yāṅṅaṉameṉiṉ; oruvarkku nālaintu muṟai muyaṉṟum aṟituyilai varuvittal kūṭātāyiṉ, orukiḻamai aṉṟi iraṇṭukiḻamai varaiyil viṭāmal nāṭōṟum muyaṉṟuvariṉ avarkku ataṉai varuvittu viṭalām. aṟituyiliṟ cella māṭṭātavar oruvarum illaiyākaiyāl, ellārkkum ataṉai varuvikkalām eṉpatu tiṇṇam. āṉāl, oru cilarkku uṭaṉēyum, vēṟu cilarkku iraṇṭu nāḷiṉum, maṟṟuñ cilarkku oru kiḻamaiyilum piṉṉuñ cilarkku iraṇṭu kiḻamaiyilum ataṉait toṭarpāka muyaṉṟu varuvikkalām mēlum, tuyiṟṟuvōr aṟituyil varuvikkum muṟaikaḷil naṉku paḻakip paḻakit tērntiruppārāyiṉ, avarkku appaḻakkattāl avvāṟṟal mika muṟuki niṟkum. attakaiyavar oruvaraiyō aṉṟi ōr āyiravaraiyō ōriṭattil oru noṭippoḻutil aṟituyiliṟ celuttamāṭṭuvar.
aṅṅaṉam atu kūṭumāyiṉum, aṟituyiliṟ cellac ciṟitum viruppamillātavarkku ataṉai varuvittu avarait tamvaḻippaṭuttumāṟu eṅṅaṉameṉiṉ; ovvoruvarkkum nāṭōṟum iyaṟkaiyāṉa tūkkam varuvatu uṇṭākaiyāl, avar aṟiyāmalēyē avariṭañ ceṉṟu, atiliruntu avarai aṟituyilukkup pōkac ceyyalām. ataṟkuriya muṟai yāteṉiṉ; ataṉaiyuṅ kāṭṭuvām; aṟituyiliṟ cella viruppamillāta oruvar iyaṟkaiyāṉa tūkkattil naṉṟāy ayarntuṟaṅkumpōtu, tuyiṟṟuvōr ētōr aravamuñ ceyyāmal avar pakkattil amaitiyāyc cellal vēṇṭum. ceṉṟu mikavun tāḻnta kuralilē avarai nōkkit tūṅkum, naṉṟāy ayarntu tūṅkum, añcavēṇṭām, nīr viḻikkakkūṭātu, āḻnta ayarnta tūkkattiṟ cellum, tūkkam, tūkkam, ayarnta tūkkam, āḻnta tūkkam eṉat tiruppit tiruppip palakāl uṟutti aḻuttamāy metuvākac colluka. iṅṅaṉañ collukaiyil, avar viḻitteḻum aṭaiyāḷaṅ kaḷ kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ, appōtu ataṉai niṟuttiviṭuka. appaṭi yallāviṭṭāl, umatu valatu kaiyāl avaratu talaiyait toṭṭu `eṉatu kural oliyait tavira vēṟu enta oliyaiyum nīr kēṭkamāṭṭīr; nīr mikavum ayarntu āḻntu uṟaṅkukiṉṟīr eṉṟu colluka. ataṉ piṟaku avarai nōkki ētēṉuṅ kēḷvi kēṭkalām. `ï¥nghJ kÂ v‹d? eṉṟāyiṉum, `ippōtu nīr oru mullaippūvai mukarukiṉṟīr, ataṉ maṇam eppaṭiyirukkiṉṟatu? eṉṟāyiṉum, viṉavuka. avaṟṟiṟku avar viṭai collāma lirupparāyiṉ, viṭai kūṟumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttu uraikka. piṉṉar viṭaikūṟuvar. maṇi iṉṉateṉṟāvatu, mullaippū naṉṟāy maṇakkiṉṟa teṉṟāvatu avar moḻikuvarāyiṉ, avar viḻittuk koḷḷāmal niṉaivaṟṟa aṟituyililētāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu uṟutippaṭuttikkoḷka. ataṉ piṉṉum āḻnta tūkkattiṟkuc cellumpaṭiyākac ciṟitu nēraṅkaṭṭurai kūṟi, appuṟam avarai numvaḻippaṭuttik koḷvataṟku evvevaṟṟaic collal vēṇṭumō, avaikaḷai ellām avarai nōkkik kaṭṭuraittuk kūṟuka. ataṉ piṟaku, avarai eḻupputaṟkumuṉ `nīr viḻittuk koṇṭapiṉ yāṉ ippōtu colliyavaikaḷai aṟiyamāṭṭīr; āyiṉum, ippōtu yāṉ colliyapaṭiyē nīr eppōtum naṭappīr eṉṟu uṟuttic colliviṭṭu, iṉi, nīr iyaṟkaiyāṉa tūkkattil tūṅki viḻikka vēṇṭum nērattil viḻittuk koḷvīr eṉṟu muṭittuk kūṟi appuṟam akalka.
ivvāṟaṉṟi maṟṟoru muṟaiyālum, iyaṟkait tūkkattiliruppōrai, aṟituyiliṟ celuttalām. aḵtāvatu: mēṟkāṭṭiyapaṭi avar arukiṟ ceṉṟavuṭaṉ vāyāṟ kaṭṭurai kūṟuvatai niṟuttik kaikaḷāl avarait toṭāmalē mukkāl viraṟkaṭai akalaniṟuttit taṭavutalālēyām. iru kaikaḷiṉ kaṭaic ciṟuviralkaḷaiyum akala niṟutti, maṟṟa viralkaḷaic ciṟitu neruṅka vaḷaittuk kīḻnōkkip piṭittu, uccantalaiyi liruntu mukattiṉ mēlākak kīḻiṟaṅki neñcuvaraiyiṟ kīḻnōkkit taṭavip, piṉṉarun taṭavutaṟkuk kaikaḷai uyarttuṅkāl appaṭiyē uyarttāmaṟ pakkac cāyvāyt tiruppi uyartti, maṟupaṭiyum uccan talaiyiliruntu neñcu varaiyil toṭāmal mukkāl viraṟkaṭai kaikaḷai eṭṭa vaittut taṭavuka; iṅṅaṉam pattu allatu patiṉaintu nimiṭaṅkaḷ varaiyil taṭaviyapiṉ, mika melliya kuralil `tūkkam, tūkkam, āḻnta tūkkam, ayarnta tūkkam, nīr mikavum ayarntu tūṅkukiṉṟīr eṉṟu kaṭṭuraikaḷaip palakāl metuvākac colluka. itu muṭintapiṉ, metuvāka avaratu neṟṟiyait toṭṭu avarai numvaḻippaṭuttutaṟkuc colla vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum mēṟkāṭṭiyapaṭiyē ceytu appāṟcelka. ivvāṟu ceytalāl, tammōṭu iṇaṅkāmal tamakku evvaḷavu māṟupaṭṭu niṟpōraiyun, tam viruppappaṭi naṭakkac ceyyalām.
immuṟaikaḷāl, nāṭpaṭṭa koṭiya nōykaḷaiyun tīrkkalām; piḷavaik kaṭṭikaḷālum āṟāta puṇkaḷālum allum pakalun tuṉpuṟuvōrkku, attuṉpañ ciṟitun teriyātapaṭi avaikaḷai aṟuttu āṟṟiyum viṭalām. ivaṟṟiṟku uṇmaiyāka nikaḻnta cila nikaḻccikaḷai iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām. aṟituyilai varuvittu, ataṉāl oruvarait tam viruppappaṭi naṭakkac ceyyalām eṉpatai, im muṟaikaḷiṟ peritun tērccipeṟṟu, ivaṟṟaik kāḷikaṭṭattil (Calcutta) uḷḷa āṅkila maruttuvak kaḻakattil nōyāḷikaḷpāṟ ceytu pārttu, ulakiṟ paravacceyta āṅkila maruttuvar oruvar (Dr. James Esdaile, M.D.) tām nērē kaṇṭu eḻutiya oru nikaḻcciyiliruntu naṉku aṟiyalāmātalāl, avareḻutiya appakutiyai iṅkē moḻi peyarttu varaikiṉṟām:-
iraṇṭu kiḻamaikaḷukkumuṉ ūkuḷik kaṭait teruviṉūṭē (Hooghly bazaar) vaṇṭiyiṟ celkaiyil, aṅkuḷḷa ūr kāvaṟ cāvaṭikku (Police Office) etirē oru tiraṇṭa kūṭṭamāṉa āṭkaḷ niṟkak kaṇṭēṉ. aṅkē naṭappatu eṉṉaveṉṟu yāṉ kēṭka, oru periya āḷ oru ciṟuvaṉait tiruṭikkoṇṭu pōkaiyiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭāṉeṉṟum, kāvaṟkūṭattiṉuḷḷē avviruvarum irukkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum cilar coṉṉārkaḷ. itaik kēṭṭatum, yāṉ akkūṭattiṉuṭ ceṉṟu, pattu allatu paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭuḷḷa oru ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉai mīṭṭavaṉākac collap paṭṭa ōr āṇmakaṉ maṭimītu amarntirukkak kaṇṭēṉ. antap paiyaṉ araivāci mayakka muṭaiyavaṉāyum, avaṉ kaṇkaḷil oṉṟu vīkkamuṭaiyatāyum iruntamaiyāl avaṉai maruttuvak kaḻakattiṟku eṭuttuc cellumpaṭi ēviṉēṉ. ataṉ piṟaku avaṉait tiruṭicceṉṟa kaḷvaṉai eṉṉiṭaṅ kāṭṭiṉar.
avaṉ tāṉ ōr ampaṭṭaṉ eṉṟāṉ; avaṉ toḻilukkuriya karuvikaḷ aṭaṅkiya aṭaippum oṉṟu eṉ etirē koṇarntu vaikkappaṭṭatu. itaṉai nāṉ karuttāy ārāyntu pārttēṉ; āṉāl, avai ampaṭṭarkkuriya vaḻakkamāṉa karuvikaḷākavē iruntaṉa. antap paiyaṉō viraivil taṉ uṇarvu taṉakku varappeṟṟavaṉākip piṉvaruṅ kataiyai eṉṉiṭañ coṉṉatum allāmal, tirumpa naṭunilai maṉṟattilum naṭuvarkku etirilum aṅṅaṉamē ciṟitum picakāmaṟ coṉṉāṉ. acciṟuvaṉ, tāṉ aṉṟu viṭiyaṟkālaiyil oru vīṭṭiṟku arukiluḷḷa vayalaṇṭai ceṉṟa ciṟitu nērattiṟkup piṟaku, pāṭṭaiyiṟ ceṉṟa oru putumaiyāṉa āṇmakaṉ atiliruntu taṉkiṭṭa vantaṉareṉṟum, kiṭṭavantatum cilar mantira moḻikaḷaic collat toṭaṅkiṉa ṉeṉṟum, ataṉpiṉ avaṉ taṉ kaikaḷaip piṭittukkoṇṭu taṉ kaṇkaḷiṉ mēṟ kuṟukkākak kaiyait taṭavat tāṉ uṇarvu mayaṅki viṭa avaṉ taṉṉai vaḻinaṭattic ceṉṟatumaṭṭum niṉaivilirukkiṉṟa teṉṟum, avaṉatu valukkaṭṭāyam iṉṟiyētāṉ avaṉaip piṉpaṟṟic cellavēṇṭiyatāyiṟ ṟeṉṟum pukaṉṟāṉ. maṟupaṭiyum taṉ uṇarvu taṉakku varappeṟṟatu, tāṉ avvāṇmakaṉai etirppaṭṭa iṭattiṟku iraṇṭu mail tolaiviluḷḷa cantiranākūrk kōṭṭai vāyilaṇṭaiyēyām eṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ.
ivvaḷavutāṉ avaṉ colliyatu. anta āḷōṭukūṭa varukaiyil acciṟuvaṉ ētoṉṟum uṇṇavumillai, parukavumillai; antap paiyaṉukku uṭaiyavaṉum nēcarkaḷum acciṟuvaṉ oḻuṅkāṉa naṭakkaiyun tiṟamaiyum uḷḷavaṉeṉavum, icippāvatu tūkkattil naṭakkum vaḻakkamāvatu avaṉukku orukālum illaiyeṉavum moḻintārkaḷ. antap paiyaṉai mīṭṭavaṉeṉṟu collappaṭṭa maṟṟōr āṭavaṉai ārāyntēṉ; avaṉ acciṟuvaṉai aṉṟu kālaiyil tāṉ pārkkaiyil avaṉ oru putumaiyāṉa āḷaip piṉ ṟoṭarntu cella, ataṟku muṉṉamē tāṉ ap paiyaṉai naṉṟāyt terintavaṉātalāl, avaṉai vaḻiyil niṟutti `nī eṉṉa ceykiṟāy? eṉṟu kēṭṭum acciṟuvaṉ ētoru viṭaiyum pakarāmal mayaṅkiyirukkak kaṇṭu, tāṉ veruṇṭu, taṇṇīraik koṇṭuvantu avaṉatu mukattiṉ mēṟ ṟeḷittum, vēṟucila māṟṟukaḷaic ceytum avaṉukku ammayakkan teḷiya vaittatākavum, maṟupaṭiyun tāṉ appoṭiyaṉai viṉāva, avaṉ tāṉ aṅkuvantatu ēṉ eṉṟu taṉakkut teriyavillai. tāṉ muṉṉaṟiyāta apputiyavaṉai eppaṭiyō piṉṟoṭarntu cellalāyiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟiyavuṭaṉ nilattiṉmēl viḻuntu kaṇṇaik kāyappaṭuttik koṇṭa tākavum eṭutturaittāṉ. itaṟkiṭaiyil, apputiyaṉ avviṭattaiviṭṭu ōṭukaiyiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu ūkuḷikkuk koṇarappaṭṭāṉ.
ataṉ piṟaku avvampaṭṭaṉai uḷḷē kūppiṭṭēṉ; avaṉ coṉṉatāvatu; antap paiyaṉ aḻutukoṇṭu mayakkattō ṭirukkaiyil avaṉait tāṉ pāṭṭaiyil etirppaṭṭatākavum, avaṉukku yātu tuṉpam nērntateṉṟu tāṉ viṉava avaṉtāṉ vaḻitappi vantu viṭṭataic coṉṉatākavum, atu kēṭṭu ūrkkāvaṟ cāvaṭikkut taṉṉōṭu kūṭavantāl aṅkiruntu oru kāvaṟkāraṉ avaṉai vīṭṭiṟ koṇṭupōyc cērppaṉeṉa moḻintatākavum pukaṉṟāṉ.
entap pakkam uṇmaiyō aḵtirukkaṭṭum; viyappāṉa icceyti eṉkaruttaik kavarntatu; av āḷiṉatu toḻilum eṉakku aiyattai eḻuppi viṭṭatu. inta nāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa ampaṭṭarkaḷ tamatu toḻilai metuvākac ceykaiyil āṭkaḷait tūṅkacceyva tuṇṭeṉṟu yāṉ kēḷvip paṭṭiruntēṉ.
tammai mayakkiviṭṭa āṇpālāriṉ piṉṉē mayakkuṇṭa makkaḷ toṭarntuceṉṟa ceytikaḷ innāṭeṅkum paraviyirukkiṉṟaṉa; aṅṅaṉam mayaṅkic ceṉṟavarkaḷ perumpālum peṇmakkaḷēyām. ulaka meṅkumuḷḷa ampaṭṭarkaḷ kūrtta aṟivum etaṉaiyum uṟṟunōkkum iyalpum uḷḷavarkaḷ. avarkaḷatu toḻilāṉatu aṟituyil varuvittaṟku ēṟṟa uṇarcciyuṭaiya uṭampiṉ mēṟpuṟattai avarkaḷ toṭutaṟku iṭañ ceykiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ mikap paḻaiya kālattilēyē avarkaḷ aṟituyilākiya maṟai poruḷait terintiruttal vēṇṭum; avarkaḷiṉ kaittoḻilukkuriya putaiporuḷāy aḵtu avarkaṭku vantirukkavēṇṭum. ippōtu nērnta im muṭṭup pāṭṭiṉiṉṟu veḷippaṭutaṟku iraṇṭu vaḻikaḷtām eṉakkut teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa; appaiyaṉiṭattuk kāṇappaṭṭa mayakkam avaṉukku iyaṟkaiyāka varum naṭaittuyi (Somnambulism) lākavātal, aṉṟic ceyaṟkaiyāka varuvikkappaṭṭa naṭait tuyilākavātal iruttal vēṇṭum.
atu, piṟkūṟiyatāka iruntāl aṟituyiliṉ muṟaikaḷāl aṉṟi vēṟeṅṅaṉam atu varuvikkappaṭum?  taṟceyalāka yāṉ innikaḻcciyaik kāṇa nērntamaiyāl, iṅṅaṉam ōr āḷaik kavarntu koṇṭu cellum muṟai irukkakkūṭumeṉpataip paṟṟic cāṉṟupakara yāṉ aḻaikkappaṭuvēṉeṉa muṉṉatākavē aṟintukoṇṭēṉ; naṭaittuyilaip paṟṟiyatoṉṟum yāṉ aṟintatillāmaiyāl, mutalil eṉakkuḷḷa kuṟaiyait tīrttukkoḷḷumporuṭṭu yāṉ ataṉai ārāya vēṇṭiṉēṉ. iḵtu aṟituyiṉ muṟaikaḷāl varuvikkak kūṭiyatāyiṟ periyatōr āṟṟal ciṟiyataṉait taṉṉuḷ aṭakki niṟṟalpaṟṟi, ataṉaip pōṟ ceyaṟkaiyāṉa naṭaittuyilai yāṉ varuvikka vallaṉāvēṉ eṉa eṇṇiṉēṉ. immuṟaiyāl aṭaiyakkūṭumāyiṉ, iyaṟkai naṭaittuyilaippōṟ ceyaṟkaiyāṉa toṉṟai varuvittaṟporuṭṭu, uṇarvaṟṟa nilaiyil oruvaraik koṇṭu cellutaṟku iṭaiyē yāṉ niṉṟuviṭal vēṇṭum.
ākavē, yāṉ ciṟaikkōṭṭattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa maruttuvak kaḻakattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa oruvaṉai aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytēṉ. ivaṉukkuk kaṇṇerivu tīrttal vēṇṭi ivaṉai itaṟkumuṟ palakāl niṉaivaṟṟa aṟituyiliṟ celuttiyirukkiṉṟēṉ; akkālaṅkaḷiṉellām uṇarvaṟṟa nilaiyiṉaḷavukku avaṉai aṟituyiliṟ koṇṭu ceṉṟu, mikak kuṟaivāyk kātukēṭkavum uṭampacaiyavum maṭṭum avaṉukku vallamai ciṟitu viṭṭuk koṭuttēṉ. ippōtu avaṉukku niṉaivaṟṟa aṟituyilai varuvikkaiyiṟ, ciṟituṇarvukku iṭam viṭṭa innilaiyil vantu avaṉai viṭalāyiṉēṉ. avaṉum annaṭaittuyilil naṭakkat tuvaṅki,akkaḻakattiṉ maṟṟoru muṉaivaraiyiṟ ceṉṟu cuvaraṇṭaipōy niṉṟāṉ; piṟaku aṅkiruntu tirumpi orē nērākac ceṉṟu maṟupaṭiyum maṟṟoru taṭaiyaṇṭai vantu muṭṭuppaṭṭu niṉṟāṉ. ciṟitu nēram yāṉ avaṉai acaiviṉṟi niṟkac ceyyavē, avaṉukku attuyil iṉṉum mikutiyāyiṟṟu; avaṉ ōcaikaḷai uṇaramāṭṭāvaṉāyiṉāṉ. avaṉuṭaiya kaṇkaḷiṉ mēl ūtiṉamaiyiṉālum, neṭuka avaṉuṭaṉ pēciṉamaiyiṉālum avaṉ attuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuntu āṅkilam intuttāṉam eṉṉum irumoḻikaḷaiyum yāṉ kūṟiyavāṟē tiruttamākat tiruppic coṉṉāṉ. avaṉai nāṉ eḻuppiyapiṟaku, tūkkattiltāṉ ceytavaikaḷaic ciṟitum aṟiyavillai yeṉṟum, avviṭattaiviṭṭut tāṉ appuṟam peyaravillai yeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ; āṉāl, akkaḻakattil yāṉ avaṉai aṟituyiliṟ celuttat toṭaṅkiya iṭattiṟku nēretirē ippōtiruntāṉ. piṟaku, nāṉ naṭunilai maṉṟattiṟkuc cāṉṟu pakarumāṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭēṉ.
“aṅkē ceṉṟatum, naṭuvar eṉṉai nōkkic `cāṉṟu kūṟiṉōr pakarntapaṭi oruvaraik kavarntu koṇṭu pōtal naṭakkakkūṭiyatutāṉ eṉṟu tāṅkaḷ eṇṇukiṉṟīrkaḷā? eṉṟu kēṭṭār.`atu naṭakkakkūṭiyatutāṉ eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ; ēṉeṉṟāl, ataippōṉṟa toṉṟai ippōtu tāṉ ceytu vantēṉ; taṉṉaiyaṟiyāmalē oru ciṟaiyāḷi eṉṉaip piṉṟoṭāntu maruttuvak kaḻakattaic cuṟṟivarac ceytēṉ. eṉṟu viṭai pakarntēṉ.
atu kēṭṭu naṭuvar avvaḻakkil akappaṭṭavaṉaic ciṟaikku aṉuppiṉār. piṉṉar avvaḻakku mēlnaṭumaṉṟattil tirumpavum ārāyccikkuvara, appōtu ataṉai ārāynta caṭṭanūl vallārkku yāṉ karutiya poruḷait tiṉaiyaḷavukūṭa viḷaṅkappaṭuttal iyalavillai. ataṉāl, am mēl naṭunilaiyar, yāṉ moḻintapaṭi, oruvar tammai yaṟiyāmalē maṟṟoruvaraip piṉṟoṭarntu cellumāṟu ceyyakkūṭumeṉpatai ac caṭṭanūl vallārkku nērē ceytu kāṭṭuvatil ētēṉun taṭaiyuṇṭōveṉak kēṭṭār. ataṉai ārāyntu kāṭṭa viruppam uṭaiyēṉ eṉṟālum, ataṉai muṭittuc ceyvēṉeṉṟu uṟuti pukala māṭṭēṉeṉṟu viṭai kūṟiṉēṉ. nammuṭaiya nōkkaṅkaḷ iṉṉaveṉṟu teriviyāmal, yāṉ peyar kūṟiya mūvar āṇpālārai ammaṉṟattiṟku varuvikka am mēlnaṭuvar kaṭṭaḷai tantāl, etu ceyyakkūṭiyatō ataṉai yāṉ ceytu pārppēṉ eṉṟēṉ.
iraṇṭoru nāḷil am mēlnaṭuvariṉ maṉṟattiṟku varumāṟu vēṇṭikkoḷḷap paṭṭēṉ; appōtu aḵtu airōppiyarālum uḷnāṭṭavarālum niṟaintiruntatu. nācir makamatu eṉpavaṉ uḷḷē koṇṭuvantu aṭaippiṉuḷḷē niṟuttappaṭṭāṉ; ciṟitu nērattil yāṉ avaṉukku aṟituyilai varuvittu viṟaikkacceyta kaikaḷōṭu avaṉai ammaṉṟattiṟku veḷiyē naṭattic ceṉṟu, ciṟitu tolaivākap pāṭṭaiyiṉmēl avaṉai naṭakkavuñ ceytēṉ; mēlum, yāṉvēṇṭiya nēram varaiyil avaṉuṭaiya kaikaḷaip pala vakaiyāṉa nilaikaḷil viṟaittu niṟkavuñ ceytēṉ. maṟupaṭiyum avaṉaik koṇṭuvantu ammaṉṟattiṉ aṭaippiṉkaṇ niṟuttiṉēṉ; appōtu, mēlnaṭuvarum caṭṭanūl vallunarum avaṉōṭu evvaḷavō pēcippārttum avaṉ avarkaḷai eḷḷaḷavum uṉṉikkavē yillai; ataṉāl avaṉai eḻuppi viṭumpaṭi avarkaḷ eṉṉaik kēṭkavēṇṭiyavarāṉārkaḷ. yāṉum aṅṅaṉamē ceytēṉ. `m›Él¤ijÉ£L btËna br‹widah? eṉṟu aṅkirunta caṭṭanūlār viṉava, `illai eṉṟu uṟutiyākac coṉṉāṉ. avarkaḷ avaṉōṭu uraiyāṭik koṇṭu irukkaiyil, avaṉ kāṇātapaṭi yāṉ avaṉukkuppiṉṉē ceṉṟu, avaṉ pēcikkoṇṭu cellum nilaiyilēyē avaṉukku aṟituyilai varuvittēṉ. appuṟam avaṉ pēcamāṭṭāta vaṉāyiṉāṉ; evaruṭaiya kuralum avaṉukkuk kēṭkavillai; maṟupaṭiyum yāṉ avaṉ kaṇkaḷiṉmēl ūti avaṉai viḻikkacceytēṉ.
ataṉpiṉ  mātūp eṉpavaṉaik koṇarntu aṭaippiṉkaṇ niṟuttiṉar. nāṉ uḷnuḻaintatai avaṉ pārkkavillai. mēl naṭuvaruñ caṭṭanūlārum avaṉait tammukappaṭuttip pēcikkoṇṭiruntaṉar; avaṉ aṟivōṭu kiḷarcciyākap pēcukaiyil yāṉ avaṉukkup piṉṉē ceṉṟu avaṉai viṟaikkac ceytēṉ; kaṭavuḷai nōkki āṇaiyiṭṭuc collum ciṟaiyāḷikaḷiṉ vaḻakkappaṭi, avaṉ collik koṇṭu varukaiyil oru noṭip poḻutil pēcavum colvataik kātāṟ kēṭkavum māṭṭātavaṉāy iṭaiyē niṉṟu pōṉāṉ. kātu kēṭka māṭṭātavaṉāy niṉṟa piṟakum, avaṉ vāyitaḻkaḷ pēcutaṟku acaivaṉapōṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu avaṉukku etiriliruntōr eṉakkuc colliṉar. acaiyum āṟṟal muḻutum aṟṟuppōka avaṉ avvaḷavu viṟaittup pōṉamaiyāl, avaṉai naṭakkumpaṭi ceytaṟkākap piṉṉēyiruntu eṉ viralkaḷāl yāṉ avaṉait taḷḷa vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu; iṭarppāṭṭōṭu avaṉ ciṟitu vaḻi naṭantāṉ; tirumpavum avaṉ muṭiyiliruntu aṭivaraiyil caṭutiyil viṟaittamaiyāl yāṉ avaṉai mella muṉṉukku unta, mikavun tiṭukkiṭattakka vakaiyāy avaṉ nilattiṉmītu talaikīḻāy viḻuntuviṭṭāṉ; mikaviraivil avaṉ viṟaittatai yāṉ aṟiyāmaṟ pōṉēṉ. ciṟitu varuttappaṭṭu yāṉ avaṉai eḻuppa vēṇṭuva tāyiṟṟu; nallakālamāy avaṉ kāyam aṭaiyavillai.
ataṉpiṟaku corūpacantaṉ eṉpavaṉ koṇarap paṭṭāṉ. yāṉ avaṉai oru tiṅkaḷākap pārāmaiyāl, avaṉatu nalattaip paṟṟiyum piṟavaṟṟaippaṟṟiyum ucāviṉēṉ; avvāṟu ucāvukaiyilēyē avaṉukku aṟituyil varuvikkum muṟaiyaic ceytēṉ. ciṟitu nērattilellām avaṉ viṭaikūṟa iyalātavaṉāṉāṉ. uṭaṉē avaṉai aṭaippi ṉiṉṟum veḷippaṭutti, viṟaitta kaikaḷōṭu pamparampōl avaṉ cuḻaṉṟuvaracceytu, muḻutum uṇarvaṟṟa nilaiyilēyē avaṉai muṉṉirunta iṭattil vantirukkappaṇṇiṉēṉ. avaṉ kātiṟ kēṭkumpaṭiyākavāvatu, uyirōṭirukkum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷai avaṉ kāṭṭumpaṭiyākavāvatu, ceyya evarālum iyalavillai. āyiṉum, yāṉ avaṉuṭaiya kaṇkaḷiṉmēl ūtiya vuṭaṉē, avaṉ taṉ aimpoṟiyuṇarvu kaḷum varappeṟṟut tāṉ avviṭattai viṭṭu akaṉṟa tillaiyeṉak kūṟiṉāṉ.
anta ampaṭṭaṉ aṟituyil muṟaiyāl acciṟuvaṉaik kavarntukoṇṭu pōyiṉaṉō allatappaṭiyillaiyō yāṉ ataṉait tīrmāṉikkap pukavillai; āyiṉum, avvāṟu ceyyakkūṭum eṉpataṉaip palarum naṉku aṟiyumāṟu meyppaṭuttik kāṭṭutaṟku eṉakku aḵtu ōr amayantantatu; yāṉ mūṉṟu peyaraip pala muṉṉilaiyiṟ kavarntu ceṉṟataṉai evarum maṟukkat tuṇiyavillai. nāṭṭupparikāri oruvaṉ evvaḷavu eḷitāy ataṉaic ceytalkūṭumō, avvaḷavukku yāṉum ōr āṭavaṉaiyāvatu allatoru peṇmakaḷai yāvatu allatu oru ciṟupiḷḷaiyaiyāvatu kavarntu cellakkūṭum. aṟituyiliṉ āḻattai yāṉ nērē kaṇṭaṟinta kālam mutal ataṉ vallamaiyāl tīmaiyum viḷaiyum naṉmaiyum viḷaiyumeṉṟu tiṭṭamāy uṇarappeṟṟēṉ. ataṉāl uṇṭām naṉmai tīmaikaḷiṉ uṇarcciyai ellār maṉattilum paṭumpaṭi ceyyum nōkkattiṉālēyē ataṉai avvaḷavu āḻamākac ceṉṟu ārāyntu pārttēṉ. aṟivu viḷakkattiṉ poruṭṭākavum nōytīrkkum poruṭṭākavum allāmal, immuṟaiyai vēṟu tīyavakaikaḷiṟ payaṉ paṭuttuvārai ellāruṅ kaṭintu collum nāḷiṉvaravu tolaivil illaiyeṉṟu nāṉ nampukiṉṟēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam uṇmaiyāka nikaḻnta innikaḻcciyaik koṇṭu aṟituyiṉ muṟaikaḷiṟ paḻakit tērccipeṟṟa oruvar tām vēṇṭiyavāṟellām piṟarai naṭakkac ceyya vallunar āvar eṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō? mēlē kāṭṭiya turaimakaṉār terivittapaṭi, koṭiyōrait tiruttit tām virumpiya naṉmaikaḷukkut tiruppik koḷḷavum, palavēṟu piḻaipāṭāṉa vaḻikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu aṟivilarāy oḻukit tuṉpuṟuvārkku aṟivuteruṭṭi avarai attuṉpattiṉiṉṟu viṭuvittuk koḷḷavum, maruntukaḷiṉun tīrāk kaṭunōykaḷait tīrttuk koḷḷavum ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟaiyiṉaip payaṉpaṭuttuvatu mika naṉṟu. in naṉṉeṟi yallāta piṟavaṟṟil itaṉaip payaṉpaṭuttaṟku oru ciṟitum niṉaittal ākātu.
maṟṟōr airōppiya turaimakaṉār (Carl Sextus, see his admirable book on hypnotism, p. 241) ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟaikaḷil mikavun tērcci peṟṟiruntār. avar vaṭa amerikkāviluḷḷa cikkākō nakarattiṟ ceṉṟu palarai aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytu viyappāṉa palakāṭcikaḷaik kāṭṭiṉār. annakarattil oru pattirikaikku āciriyarāṉa paṇṭi eṉpavar tam ēvaṟkāraṉ oruvaṉ poruttup piṭippālum kāyccalālum varun tiṉamaiyiṉ, annōykaḷait tīrkkumporuṭṭu ak kalaiñarait tamatu illattiṟku varuvittār. avarum ataṟkicaintu orunāḷ muṟpakaliṟ pattumaṇikku aṅkē ceṉṟār. piṟaku tām aṅkē ceytataṉait tāmē eḻutukiṉṟār; atu varumāṟu: nāṉ avaṉatu paṭukkaiyaṟaikkuṭ pukuntatum avaṉ paṭukkaiyiṉ talaimāṭṭiṉkaṭ ceṉṟu niṉṟu, eṉ kaṇkaḷai uṟṟunōkkumpaṭi avaṉukkuk kaṟpittēṉ. nāṉku nimiṭaṅkaḷ kaḻintatum, avaṉ eṉṉuṭaiya niṉaivāṟṟaluḷ muḻutum akappaṭṭu niṉṟāṉ. piṟaku avaṉuṭaiya uṟuppukaḷaip pala nilaikaḷil niṟuttiṉēṉ; annilaikaḷai avaṉ māṟṟa muṭiyātavaṉāṉāṉ. iḵtu, avaṉ viṟaippu nilaiyiliruk kiṉṟāṉ eṉpatait teḷivuṟak kāṭṭiṟku. iraṇṭumaṇi nērattiṟkuḷ avaṉ mikavum nalamaṭaiyap peṟuvāṉeṉṟum, paṭukkaiyiṉiṉṟum eḻa virumpuvā ṉeṉṟum, ataṉpiṉ nallavuṇavai uṭkoḷvāṉeṉṟum, vaḻakkampōla avaṉ taṉ vēlaiyaic ceyyap pukuvāṉeṉṟuṅ kaṭṭurai moḻikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. piṟpakal iraṇṭu maṇikku avaṉ kiḷarcciyāyt taṉ paṭukkaiyai viṭṭeḻuntu, nalattōṭum viraiviṟ paciyeṭuttut taṉatu pakaluṇavai uṇṭāṉ. piṉṉar nāṉku maṇikkellām avaṉ muṟṟilum nalamaṭaintamaiyiṉ pāṭikkoṇṭuñ cīḻkkai aṭittukkoṇṭum taṉakku vaḻakkamāyuḷḷa vēlaiyaic ceyyavallaṉ āṉāṉ.
iṉi, itaṟku muṟkāṭṭiya eṭēl, eṉṉum āṅkila maruttuvar, koṭiya piḷavaik kaṭṭikaḷālum āṟāp puṇkaḷālum neṭunāḷ tuṉpuṟṟa nōyāḷikaḷ palarkku aṟituyilai varuvittu avarkaṭku nōy ciṟitun teriyāmalē avaṟṟai yaṟuttu nalappaṭuttiṉa varalāṟukaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai iṅṅaṉē moḻipeyarttu varaikiṉṟām: - aimpatu āṇṭuḷḷa muralitā eṉṉum oru kuṭiyāṉavaṉ ceṉṟa ōr āṇṭākak kuruttelumpaip paṟṟiya oru koṭumpuṇṇāl tuṉpuṟṟukkoṇṭiruntāṉ; appuṇ avaṉatu iṭatu kāliṉ keṇṭaic cataiyil tōlukkumēl araiviraṟkaṭai uyaramākavum āṟuviral nīḷamākavum iruntatōṭu, avaṉatu antak kālaiyum toṭaikku nērkōṇamāy niṟkumpaṭi muṭakkiviṭṭatu. mutaṉmutalāka avaṉ iṉṟaikkuttāṉ aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭṭāṉ; avaṉai attuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuppāmal vaittuk koṇṭē, paḻukkak kāycciyatōr irumpai appuṇṇiṉuḷ āḻa nuḻaittu ataṉait tīyttēṉ. maṟunāḷ ataṉaippārkka ataṉ mēṟpuṭaippu nirampat taṭippāyum aḻuttamāyum iruntamaiyāl, ataṉai akkeṇṭaic cataiyiṉiṉṟum aṟutteṭukka vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu; ākavē, avaṉukkut tirumpavum aṟituyil varuvikkappaṭṭatu. avaṉ uṇarātapaṭi cuṟṟilum uḷḷa tōliṉ maṭṭattiṟku appuṇṇiṉ puṭaippu cīvit taḷḷappaṭṭatu. itaṟkaṭuttanāḷ tirumpavum avaṉai aṟituyiliṟ kiṭattiyapōtu, muṭaṅkiya avaṉatu kālai muṟṟilum nimirttuc cirāy vaittuk kaṭṭiṉēṉ; avaṉ viḻikkavillai; piṉṉar viḻittatum avaṉukku nōvillai; eṉ muḻuvaliyum niṟaiyuṅkoṇṭu am muṭakkiṉai nimirttu kaiyil eṉ kaiyiṉkīḻ narampiṉ curuṭṭu neṟuneṟu veṉṟu piṭippu viṭuvatai yuṇarntēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam nōytīrtta āṅkila maruttuvar tamakku utaviyāṉa maṟṟoru maruttuvarai ēvi maṟṟumoru nāṭpaṭṭa nōyaip pōkkiṉa varalāṟum iṅku moḻipeyart teḻutuvām: - intiyāvil irupatu āṇṭukaḷāka irukkum kālṭar eṉṉun turai iṉṟaikku eṉṉiṭam vantu tām poruttup piṭippiṉālum kaikāl naṭukkattiṉālum taḷarcciyiṉālun tuṉpuṟuvatāka muṟaiyiṭṭukakoṇṭār. mikunta varuttattōṭu oru taṭiyai ūṉṟikkoṇṭu naṭakkiṉṟār; mettaippaṭiyiṉmēl avarāl ēṟa iyalātu. avaruṭaiya narampukaḷ nuṟuṅki viṭṭaṉa, avaratu kaṭpārvai maṅkippōy viṭṭatu, eḻutukaiyil avaratu kai naṭuṅkukiṉṟatu. maṭṭamāṉa taraiyiṉmēl naṭakkuṅkāl kīḻē vaḻukkuvatāka avarukku ōr eṇṇan tōṉṟukiṉṟatu; ataṉāl avar tammaik kāttukkoḷḷumpaṭi ceyyum muyaṟciyē avaraik kīḻē taḷḷiviṭukiṉṟatu. cilakālamāka avar tamatu vaṇṭimēliruntum ataṉaic celutta muṭivatillai; aḵtēṉ eṉṟāl, tamakketirēvarum vaṇṭiyōṭu tamatu kutiraipōymuṭṭikkoḷḷātapaṭi ceyyat tammāl ākāteṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. avar inta nilaimaiyiṟ ceṉṟa iraṇṭarai āṇṭukaḷāka iruntu varukiṟār; akkāla mellām avar ettaṉaiyō maruttuvariṭañ ceṉṟu, ēṟakkuṟaiya oru maruttuvak kaḻakattil uḷḷa maruntavvaḷavuñ cāppiṭṭār; ētoru payaṉum uṇṭākavillai. eṉ maṉaccāṉṟukku māṟāka iṉi avarukku maruntu koṭukka muṭiyāteṉṟu kūṟi, avarukku naṉmaiyai uṇṭākka nīṇṭa nāḷum poṟumaiyum vēṇṭumēṉum, avaratu nilaimai aṟituyiṉ muṟaiyāl nōytīrttaṟku ēṟṟa toṉṟākumeṉa eṇṇi, iyaṟkai nikaḻcci avarkku yātu ceyyakkūṭu meṉpatai āṇṭu pārkkumpaṭi avarkku eṭuttuc coṉṉēṉ. nōytīrttaṟku itaṟku muṟceytu pārātatoṉṟaip piṭittup pārppatil avarukku mikunta makiḻcci uṇṭāyiṟṟu; ovvōr iravum paṭukkaiyi liruntapaṭiyākavē varukku orumaṇi nēram varaiyil aṟituyil varuvikkumpaṭi eṉ tuṇaiyāṭkaḷil oruvarkkuk kaṟpittēṉ.
kālṭar eṉṉum atturaimakaṉār aṟituyiliṟ kiṭantapōtu yāṉ avarkiṭṭa orukālum irunta tillāmaiyāl, avar tamatu nilaimaiyaip paṟṟip piṉvarumāṟu eṉakku eḻutiyaṉuppiya kuṟippukkāka avarukku nāṉ kaṭamaip paṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉ.
ceṉṟa cepṭampart tiṅkaḷ ki.pi. 1845ām āṇṭaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār 25-ām nāḷ, taṅkaṭku utaviyāyuḷḷa nāṭṭu maruttuvar, tāṅkaḷkaṟpitta paṭiyākavē, eṉakku mutaṉmutal aṟituyilai varuvikka muyaṉṟār; āṉāl, aṉṟaikku atu kaikūṭavillai; ammuyaṟci oṉṟaraimaṇi nēram naṭaipeṟṟatu. 26, 27 ām nāṭkaḷilum aṅṅaṉamē; āṉāl, 28ām nāḷ atē nēram varaiyil viṭāppiṭiyāymuyaṉṟatil, yāṉ āḻntaṟi tuyiliṟ celuttappaṭṭiruntu, aintumaṇi nērattiṟku mēṟṟāṉ viḻitteḻuntēṉ. eṉ kaṇṇiṟaippaikaḷai mēl nimirttuvatilum, eṉ kaṇkaḷait tiṟantu vaippatilum yāṉ peritum iṭarppaṭṭēṉ. yāṉ eṉatu cāymaṇaikkaṭṭilai viṭṭiṟaṅki, eṉ paṭukkaikkup pōṉēṉ; maṟunāṭ kālai āṟumaṇi varaiyil yāṉ iyaṟkaiyāṉa tuyiliṟ kiṭantēṉ. oru kiḻamaikkuppiṉ, eṉṉai aṟituyiliṟ celuttum muyaṟci nāṭōṟum oru maṇi nēram varaiyil naṭaipeṟṟatu; āṉāṟ cila nimiṭam varaiyiṟ ṟūṅkac ceytaṟku mēṟ piṟitoṉṟum viḷaiyavillai. eṉṟālum, iraviṉkaṇ iyaṟkaiyāṉa tūkkattil iṉitākat tuyiṉṟu varalāṉēṉ; eṉatu nōyiṉ tuṉpam nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟak kuṟaintu varalāyiṟṟu; eṉṉuṭaiya narampukaḷ nirampavum iṟukkamuṟṟaṉa; ippōtu nāṉ utavi vēṇṭāmalē mettaip paṭikkaṭṭiṉmēl mēluṅ kīḻumāy naṭakkak kūṭiyavaṉāṉēṉ; vaṇṭiyaiyum yāṉ maṉat tiṭpattōṭu celuttak kūṭum; āṉāl iraṇṭarai āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ yāṉ iṅṅaṉañ ceyya muyaṉṟirukka muṭiyātu. akṭōpar, 17ām nāḷ varaiyil, ovvoru mūṉṟallatu nāṉkāmnāḷ iravum yāṉ iraṇṭumaṇi nēram varaiyil aṟituyiliṟ celuttap paṭṭuk kiṭantēṉ; ataṉāl viḷainta naṉmaiyai, nēṟṟuk kālaiyil yāṉ nāṉku mailukku mēlum, iṉṟaik kālaiyil āṟu mailukku mēlum naṭakka vallaṉāyiruppatu koṇṭu tāṅkaḷē terintu koḷḷalām.
itukāṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya uṇmai nikaḻccikaḷaik koṇṭu oruvarait tam vaḻippaṭuttalum, avarkkuḷḷa nāṭpaṭṭa koṭiya nōykaḷai nīkkutalum, piḷavaikkaṭṭikaḷ puṇkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai avar aṟiyāmalē aṟuttut tīrttalum niṉaivaṟṟa āḻnta aṟituyiliṉkaṇ avaraic celuttutalāl eḷitil muṭikkappaṭum eṉpatai naṉkuṇarntu koḷka.
17. teḷivukkāṭci
iṉi naṭaittuyiliṉ payaṉkaḷaippaṟṟic ciṟitu pēcuvām. naṭaittuyiliṉkaṭ celuttappaṭṭa oru tūya uyir iṟantakāla nikaḻkāla etirkāla nikaḻccikaḷait terinturaikka valla tātalai mēlē viḷakkip pōntām. innaṭait tuyilāṉatu niṉaivaṟṟa āḻnta aṟituyiliṉum āḻamāyc ceṉṟapiṉ varuvatākum. ittuyilil iruppōr atilirukkum varaiyil, iyaṟkaiyāka viḻittiruppōraip pōṟ pēcutalum naṭattaluñ ceyvarāyiṉum, avaratu niṉaivu appōtu mika nuṇṇiya aṟivuṭampaip paṟṟikkoṇṭu iyaṅkutalāl, avar iyaṟkaiyāka viḻittirukkum nilaiyiṟ kāṇamāṭṭāta, maṟainta arumperuṅ kāṭcikaḷai yellāṅ kāṇamāṭṭuvar. itu tavavoḻukkattil niṟpōrkkut tāṉākavē varuva toṉṟākaiyāl, tavakkāṭci yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ aruntavattōrkkum, ivvaṟituyilil iruppōrkkum vēṟupāṭeṉṉai yeṉiṉ; aruntavattōr tāmākavē iṭaiyaṟātu ceytu pōtarun tavamuyaṟciyāl, tamatuṇarviṉai aṟiyāmaiyiṉiṉṟum pirittu viḷaṅkacceytu, tām vēṇṭiyapōtu ivvuṇavuṭampu, uyirppuṭampu, maṉavuṭampu, aṟivuṭampu, iṉpavuṭampu  (vaṭanūlār ivaṟṟai muṟaiyē aṉaṉamayakōcam, pirāṇamayakōcam, maṉōmayakōcam, viññāṉamayakōcam, āṉantamayakōcam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar) eṉṉum aivakai yuṭampukaḷiṉum naṭaipeṟacceytu āṇṭāṇṭu nikaḻum maṟaiporuṇikaḻccikaḷai yellān tāmākavē eḷitiluṇarap peṟuvar; avarkaṭku maṟatiyum aṟiyāmaiyum uḷavākā. maṟṟup piṟar oruvaratu utaviyāl naṭaittuyiliṉkaṭ celuttappaṭuvārō tāmākavē aṟivuṭampiṟ cellamāṭṭāmaiyōṭu, āṇṭu nikaḻcci kaḷaiyun tāmākavē aṟinturaikka māṭṭuvār allar; atu vallāmalum, tām viḻittirukkum nilaiyil uṇarntavaṟṟai naṭaittuyiliṉkaṭ ceṉṟavuṭaṉē maṟantum, naṭaittuyiliṉkaṇ uṇarntavaṟṟai viḻippu nilaikku vantavuṭaṉ maṟantum aṟiyāmaiyiṟ paṟṟappaṭṭavarāyiruppar; ovvōruṭampilum vevvēṟu uyirpōl naṭappar; aruntavattiṉarō avvellā vuṭampukaḷilum ōruyirākavē oru toṭarpupaṭṭa vuṇarvuṭaiyarāyiruppar. ituvē naṭaittuyiliṉkaṭ celuttap paṭuvōrkkum, aruntavat tōrkkum uḷatākiya vēṟṟumaiyām. ātalāl, aruntavattōrkku nikaḻvatait tavakkāṭci yeṉavum, naṭaittuyilil iruppōrkku nikaḻvatait teḷivuk kāṭci yeṉavum avvēṟṟumai tōṉṟak kūṟiṉām.
iṉi, innaṭaittuyilil iruppōrkkuk kaṇkaḷ mūṭiyiruppiṉun tiṟantiruppiṉum avar kāṇpaṉa nam ūṉa viḻikaḷukku nērē pulappaṭum poruḷkaḷ alla; maṟṟu naṅ kaṇkaḷukku nērēyillāmal tolaivilum aṇimaiyilum maṟaintiruppavaikaḷēyām. maṉattūymaiyum nuṇṇaṟivu muṭaiya nam māṇavar oruvarai orukāl nām āḻnta aṟituyiliṟ celutta avaruṭaṉē naṭaittuyiliṟ ceṉṟu teḷivukkāṭci yuṭaiyarāṉār. appōtavar kaṇkaḷ mūṭiyē iruntaṉa. annilaiyil avar maṟainta poruḷkaḷaik kāṇa vallunarāy irupparā eṉat terital vēṇṭi, ematu kaikkaṭikārattai eṭuttu avaratu piṉṟalaippuṟattē piṭittu ippōtu nīr ikkaṭikārattaik kāṇkiṉṟīr; itaṉ mukattaṭṭiṉmēl maṇi muḷḷum nimiṭamuḷḷum iyaṅkuvataiyun teḷivāyk kāṇkiṉṟīr. ām, ivaṟṟaik kāṇkiṉṟīrallavā? eṉṟu yām viṉāva, avartam kaṇkaḷ naṉṟāy mūṭiyiruntum ām, oru kaik kaṭikārattaik kāṇkiṉṟēṉ; ataṉ mukattaṭṭiṉmēl muṭkaḷ  acaikiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟu avar annaṭaittuyiliruntapaṭiyē viṭai kūṟiṉār. ataṉpiṉ appaṭiyāṉāl ippōtu maṇi eṉ? collum eṉṟēm. ataṟkavar ippōtu āṟaṭikka aintu nimiṭam eṉṟu picakāmaṟ kūṟiṉār. aṉṟu mālaikkālam, avar colliyapaṭiyē oru nimiṭamum picakavillai; āṟaṭikka aintu nimiṭaṅkaḷē iruntaṉa. itukaṇṭu viyappuṟṟēm.
orukāl yām tirunelvēlikkuc ceṉṟirukkaiyil, iv vaṟituyiṉmuṟaikaḷil tērccipeṟṟa ōr aṉpar tammāl aṟituyiliṟ palakāṟ celuttappaṭṭa oru pārppaṉa iḷaiñarai emmiṭam aḻaittu vantār. atu mālaik kālam, appōtutāṉ viḷakkēṟṟappaṭṭatu. avar avviḷaiñarai oru cāymāṉak kuṟicciyil amarttic ciṟitu nērattil āḻnta naṭaittuyiliṟ pōkacceytu, ippōtu ivar teḷivukkāṭci yuṭaiyavarāyirukkiṉṟār. tāṅkaḷ ētēṉum oru poruḷaik kaiyil maṟaittu vaittuk koṇṭāl, ataṉai ivar uṭaṉē aṟintu colvar eṉṟār. yām accamayattiṟ kiṭaitta oru cāviyai atu kōkkappaṭṭa kayiṟṟōṭum eṭuttu aṅkiruntār evarkkun teriyāmal ematu valatu kaiyiṉuḷ maṟaittu vaittuk koṇṭēm. piṟaku avar aṟituyililirukkum avviḷaiñarai nōkki ippōtu nīr viḷakkukaḷaik kāṇkiṉṟīr, ellām orē veḷiccamāy irukkiṉṟaṉa. umakkup pakkattē niṟkum ivarkaḷaiyuṅ kāṇkiṉṟīr eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.
avviḷaiñarum ataṟkiṇaṅki ām, orē oḷiyā yirukkiṉṟatu. nīṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭavarkaḷaiyum pārkkiṉṟēṉ. eṉṟāl. appaṭiyāṉāl, ivarkaḷiṉ valatu kaiyiṉuḷḷē eṉṉa irukkiṉṟatu? eṉṟu av aṉpar viṉaviṉār. ataṟkavar mutalil avar kai mūṭiyirukkiṉṟatu, yāṉ uḷḷē yiruppataip pārkkakkūṭavillai. eṉṟu viṭaikūṟiṉār. maṟupaṭiyum avar, ippōtu naṉṟāy uṟṟuppārum, kaiyiṉuḷḷē yiruppatu naṉṟāyt teriyum, veḷiccam naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatu. eṉṟār. avviḷaiñar ām, avar kaiyiṉuḷḷē nīḷamāy oṉṟirukkiṉṟatu; ō, aḵtu oru ciṟu kayiṟu. eṉṟār. tirumpavum avar piṉṉum eṉṉa irukkiṉṟatu? naṉṟāy uṟṟuppārum. eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttuc coṉṉār. atil ētō oṉṟu ciṟitāyirukkiṉṟatu; āṉāl aḵtu iṉṉateṉṟu teriyavillai. eṉa moḻintār. avar illai. ippōtu atu naṉṟāyt teriyum. veḷiccam iṉṉum naṉṟāyirukkiṉṟatu; naṉṟāyp pārum eṉṟār. ām, ippōtu mikunta oḷi vīcukiṉṟatu; ō, aḵtu oru cāvi eṉṟu kaṭaiciyāka uṇmaiyaic colliviṭṭār. ataṉpiṉṉum ematu pakkattilirunta iraṇṭoruvar vēṟu cilavaṟṟai maṟaittu vaittukkoḷḷa avaikaḷaiyum avar picakāmaṟ colliviṭṭār.
aṟituyiṉ muṟaiyil mikavun tērnta oru turaimakaṉ (Carl Sextus) oru ciṟu peṇṇaikkoṇṭu neṭuntolaiyil nikaḻntatoru nikaḻcciyaic colluvittaṉar. ataṉai avar collukiṟapaṭiyē iṅku moḻipeyart teḻukiṉṟām: ēḻu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ cuvīṭaṉil uḷḷa kātaṉparkkil yāṉ iruntapōtu, pukaḻpeṟṟa celvar oruvar illattil aṟituyiluṇmaiyaippaṟṟi oru virivurai nikaḻtti, attuyilaic cilarkku varuvittuṅ kāṭṭiṉēṉ. iṅṅaṉañ ceykaiyil, mantirakkaṇṇāṭi (Magic mirror)Æš nōkkutalaik kuṟittu yāṉ pēca, accelvar ataṉaiyuñ ceytukāṭṭumpaṭi eṉṉaik kēṭṭār. appōtu avaratu kuṭumpattiṟku aṟimukamāṉavarkaḷum naṇparkaḷumāka aṅkē kūṭiyirunta irupattaintu muppatu peyaril, ammuṟaiyaic ceytu pārttaṟkut takkavar oruvarum illaiyeṉa oru noṭippoḻutiṟ kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉ aṉṟumālai pattumaṇi nēram irukkum.
itaṟkut takkavar ivvivṟu irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu yāṉ kuṟittuk kāṭṭiyapaṭi, mikavum poruttamākap pattup patiṉōrāṇṭuḷḷa orumakaḷ tammiṭam aluval pārkkum oruvarukku iruppatāka akkūṭṭattil irunta tarakar oruvar kūṟiṉār. acciṟumiyiṉ peyarum iruppiṭamum avar collavē, avaḷ arukāmaiyiliruntamaiyāl avaḷai aḻaittuvarumpaṭi viraivāka ōr āḷ aṉuppappaṭṭaṉar. avaḷ peṟṟōrkaḷ ammuṟaiyait tērntu pārttaṟkāka ētoru taṭaiyuñ collāmaiyāl, pōṉavar acciṟumiyai aḻaittu vantār. acciṟumiyai aṟituyiliṟ celutti, aṅku vaikkappaṭṭa mantirak kaṇṇāṭiyai uṟṟunōkkumpaṭi ceytōm; appeṇ viyakkattakka iyaṟkai nuṇuṇarvum ciṟanta teḷivuk kāṭciyum uṭaiyaḷāyiruntāḷ. ṭōk kōmil uḷḷa ciṟṟaraci oruttiyaik kāṇumpaṭi yāṉ avaḷukkuc coṉṉēṉ.
avaḷum ciṟitum malaippaṭaiyāmal `appaṭiyē eṉṟu uṭampaṭṭuc colli, acciṟṟaraciyiṉ vaṭivattaiyum avaḷ aṉṟu mālaiyil amarntirukkum aṟaiyaiyum nirampa naṉṟāy viritturaittāḷ. (cuvīṭaṉukkut talainakarāṉa ṭākkōm eṉpatu ippōtu yāṅkaḷirukkuṅ kātaṉpark eṉṉum nakarukku munnūṟṟaimpatu mail tolaivil uḷḷatu; icciṟumi acciṟṟaraciyai oru kālum pārttavaḷum allaḷ.) ataṉ piṟaku, acciṟumi, avvaṟaiyil avvaraciyōṭu uṭaṉirukkum ōr iḷaiñaṉaiyuṅ kaṇṭa avaṉṟaṉ uruvattiṉ taṉmai kaḷaiyum nuṭpamāka viritturaikkap pukuntāḷ.
ataik kēṭṭu akkūṭṭattil iruntavarkaḷellām nakaiyāṭiṉar. ceṉṟa āṟu tiṅkaḷāka avvaraciyiṉpāl mikunta kātalaṉpu uṭaiyaṉāy avaḷaip pārāṭṭik koṇṭu oḻukum av varaciḷaiñaṉaippaṟṟi acciṟumi paṭam eḻutik kāṭṭiṉāṟpōṟ colliya viḷakkavurai avvaḷavum ottiruntaṉa. āṉālum avaḷatu viḷakkavurai varavara aḷavukku miñcic ceṉṟamaiyālum, pulliya taṉmaiyuṭaiya tāyiruntamaiyālum yāṉ attaṉaiyōṭu ataṉai niṟutta vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu. acciṟṟaraciyaiyum avaḷ kātalaṉaiyum paṟṟiya oru kuṟippu aṅkiruntavar kēṭkat takkatāyillai; mēlum. avvillattiṟkuriya celvarkkum atu tuṉpan taruvatāyiruntatu. tāṉum taṉ naṭakkaiyum ippōtu palaruṅ kāṇattakka nilaiyiliruppataic ciṟitum uṇarātu, katavukaḷ tāḻiṭṭuc cāttappaṭṭa aṟaikkuḷ tāṉ pattiramāyiruppa taṉāl, tāṉ ūrār paḻiccollukkut tappik koṇṭatāka acciṟṟaraci eṇṇiyiruntāḷ. aṟituyilaippaṟṟi āṅkāṅku virivurai nikaḻttum poruṭṭu nārvē muḻutum nāṉku tiṅkaḷ varaiyiṟ cuṟṟivarutaṟkāka aṭutta iraviṟ kiṟittiyāṉiyā nakarattiṟkuc ceṉṟēṉ. kātaṉpark nakarattiṟku yāṉ maṟupaṭiyum tirumpivanta poḻutu muṉṉē eṉakku viruntāṟṟiya ac celvaraik kaṇṭēṉ.
naṭaittuyiliṟ ceṉṟa ciṟumi ac ciṟṟaraciyaip paṟṟit teḷivuk kāṭciyiṟ kaṇṭu kūṟiyavaikaḷaik kuṟittut tām iṭaiyē ārāyntu pārkka avai yavvaḷavum uṇmaiyā yirukkat teḷintatāka eṉakku eṭutturaittār. ataṉōṭu, aṉṟu mālaiyil ac ciṟṟaraciyiṉ naṭakkaiyaip paṟṟi ellārum naṭaiyāṭat takkatāka avaḷ muṉṉaṟintu colliya nikaḻcci oṉṟum nirampa uṇmaiyāyiruntateṉṟum avar eṉakkaṟivittār. ivvāṟu aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟa ciṟcilar teḷivuk kāṭciyuṭaiyarāyt tolaivil nikaḻum nikaḻcci kaḷaiyum nērē kāṇpatupōl teḷḷat teḷiyak kaṇṭuraikkum viyappiṉai itiṟ ciṟitu muyaṟciyeṭuppā revarun tāmēyum nēriluṇarntu viyakkalām.
iṅṅaṉam nilapparappiṉmēl aṇmaiyiluñ cēymaiyilum maṟaiviliruppaṉavaṟṟaiyum naṭappaṉavaṟṟaiyun teḷivuk kāṭciyuṭaiyār kāṇavallarātal pōlavē, innilattiṉuḷḷē mikavum āḻamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa poruḷkaḷaiyum avarkaḷ teḷiyak kaṇṭuraikka vallavarkaḷā yirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. itaṉaiyum uṇmaiyā nikaḻnta oru nikaḻcciyiṉāl viḷakkik kāṭṭuvām. iḵtu amerikkāvilmeyyāka naṭantu, kalviyiṟ perumpukaḻ peṟṟa īṭaṉ (See the article by Dr. Samuel Eadonï M.A., M.D., LL.D. Ph. D., in “The Phrenological Annual” 1892) eṉpavarāl ōr itaḻil varaiyappaṭṭatu. ataṉai aṅkiruntapaṭiyē yeṭuttu iṅku moḻipeyart teḻutukiṉṟām: - cikkākō nakaramāṉatu, ulakattil nāṭōṟum viraintu muṉṉēṟṟam aṭaiyum nakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟeṉpatu yāvarum naṉkaṟintatēyām. yōṉā eṉpavariṉ curaikkoṭi orē iravil muḷaittup paṭarntatu pōla, atuvum ōr iravil vaḷarkiṉṟatu. ataṉ kuṭimakkaḷ viraiviṟ perukiviṭṭamaiyāl, uyirvāḻkkaikkuñ celvavāḻkkaikkun taṇṇīr iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuva tāyiṟṟu. iyaṟkaip poruṇūl (Science) picakip pōyiṟṟu; nilanūlvallār (Geologists) attakaiya nilappaṭaiyiṉ kīḻt taṇṇīr iruttal kūṭāteṉṟu colliviṭṭārkaḷ. mēṟparappilētāṉ taṇṇīrkkākap pārkkavēṇṭu vatāyiṟṟu; aḻukkāṉa taṇṇīraiyē payaṉpaṭuttak koṭuttaṟku oru kūṭṭuc cērntatu.
inta nāḷil ac cikkākō nakarattil āpirakām jēm eṉṉum peyaruṭaiya oruvaṉ iruntāṉ; avaṉ kaḷaṅkam aṟṟavaṉ, kiṟittumatattiṟkup puṟampāṉavar kuṭiyiṟ piṟantavaṉ, kalviyaṟivillātavaṉ, uṇmaiyil oṉṟumēyaṟiyāta ōr āḷ. anta jēm eṉpavaṉ iyaṟkaiyākavē teḷivukkāṭci vāynta ōr uṇmaiyāṉa karuviyeṉṟum, avaṉ naṭait tuyiliṉkaṭ celuttappaṭṭapōtu annakarattiṟku aṇṭaiyiṟ kīḻē kuṟippāṉa oru nilappāṅkil ērāḷamāṉa taṇṇīrum maṇṇeṇṇeyum irukkiṉṟaṉavāka aṟivittaṉaṉeṉṟum kāroliṉ jārṭaṉ eṉṉum mātar oruvar veḷiyaṟi vittaṉar. nīṇṭakālam varaiyil avaṉ kūṟiyatai evarumē uṉṉittup pārkkavillai. kaṭaiciyākac cikkākō nakarattiṟku meyiṉ eṉṉum ūriliruntu aluvalmēlvanta uvaiṭheṭ, kāṭ eṉṉum iruvar, āpirakām jēm eṉpavaṉ kūṟiya ceytiyaik kēṭṭut tōṇṭiṉāl ērāḷamāṉa taṇṇīr kiṭaikkum iṭamāka avaṉāṟ collappaṭṭa nilattaṇṭai avaṉai aḻaittuk koṇṭu ceṉṟārkaḷ. naṭaittuyiliṟ ceṉṟatum, jēm avviṭattai uṭaṉē cuṭṭikkāṭṭiṉāṉ. tāṉ attaṇṇīraip pārppatōṭu, aḵtu, avarkaḷ appōtu niṟkum iṭattiṟku iraṇṭāyiram mailtolai vil uḷḷa malaippāṟai kaḷiliruntu tōṉṟip pala nila aṭukkukaḷiṉūṭum nilavaṟai kaḷiṉūṭum ōṭivaruvataiyun tāṉ orupaṭattileḻutik kāṭṭa kkūṭumeṉṟum avarkaṭkuc coṉṉāṉ. uṭaṉē annilattai vilaikoṭuttu vāṅkum ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytu, ataṉait tōṇṭutaṟkut tuvaṅkiṉar. iṅṅaṉan tuvaṅkiyatu ki.pi 1864ām āṇṭu pipravarit tiṅkaḷilēyākum; navampart tiṅkaḷ varaiyil nāṭōṟum nilattait tōṇṭikkoṇṭē pōka, eḻunūṟṟup patiṉōraṭi āḻattiṟ ceṉṟatun taṇṇīr vantu taṭṭiyatu; uṭaṉē irupattunāṉku maṇi nērattiṟku munnūṟāyiram marakkāl taṇṇir veḷikkiḷampi ōṭalāyiṟṟu.
tamarūci tuḷaittuc ceṉṟa pottalāṉatu kīḻuḷḷa nilappaṭaikaḷiṉ vakaikaḷaik kāṭṭiyatu; ippaṭaikaḷ attaṉaiyum muṉṉamē teḷivukkāṭciyuṭaiya avaṉ kaṇṭu coṉṉamaiyāl, nērē kāṇum maṟṟavar kaṇkaḷukku avai ataṉ uṇmaiyai meypeṟak kāṭṭiṉa . mutal nūṟu aṭivaraiyil tamarūci tuḷaittuc ceṉṟatu aṭaimaṇ paṭaiyāyiruntatu; ataṟkuk kīḻē muppattaintu aṭivaraiyil eṇṇeyōṭu ūṟiya cukkāṉkaṟ paṭaiyāyiruntatu, antac cukkāṉkal evvakaiyāṉa nilakkariyaiyumpōl naṉṟāy eriyum; ataṟkuṅ kīḻē nūṟu aṭivaraiyil calavaikkaṟpaṭai; ataṟkuṅkīḻē nūṟṟirupattaintu aṭivaraiyiṟ cempup poṭiyum irumpup poṭiyuṅ kalantu cakkimukkik kallum maṇalum oruṅku tiraṇṭa paṭai; ataṟkuṅ kīḻē nūṟṟaim pattāṟu aṭikāṟum maṇṇeṇṇey kalantu koḻuppup pōl taṭippāyum nuṇṇiya cuṇṇāmpu pōl melliya tāyumuḷḷa vaṇṭal mēlē varappeṟṟa kaṟpāṟaippaṭai; ataṉaiyaṭuttu ainnūṟṟu muppatu aṭikāṟum īyaṅkalanta cukkāṉkaṟpaṭai; piṟaku aṟunūṟṟu muppattoṉpataṭi varaiyil kantakattōṭu kalanta irumpum cakkimukkik kallum uḷḷa cukkāṉkaṟpaṭai mikavum aḻuttamāyiruntamaiyāl ataṉait turuvikkoṇṭu cellum vēlai metuvāy naṭaipeṟṟatu. intanērattil nilattiṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa āvikaḷ mēleḻumpu taliṉāl oru kontaḷippu aṭikkaṭi uṇṭāyiṟṟu; taṇṇīr caṭutiyil muppatu aṭiyiliruntu aṟupatu aṭi kīḻēpōyt tamarūciyiṉ ciṟutuṇṭukaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum eṭuttuk koṇṭu maṟupaṭiyuñ caṭutiyil mēlēvantatu. iṉṉun, tuḷaikkum vēlai naṭantu koṇṭē pōyiṟṟu; piṉṉar eḻunūṟṟup patiṉōraṭi āḻamuḷḷa oru malaippāṟaiyiṉ vaḷaivu muḻutun tuḷaikkap paṭṭavuṭaṉē, aṭiyil nālarai viraṟkaṭai akalamuḷḷa ōr uruvutuḷaiyi liruntu mikak kuḷirnta taṇṇīrāṉatu paḷiṅkupōṟ ṟeḷivuṭaiyatāy, vairampōṟ ṟūyatāy, ūṉkaḻivu puṟpūṇṭukaḷiṉ kaḻivu etuvum oruciṟituṅ kalavātatāyp, parukutaṟkum uṭampiṉ nalam pēṇutaṟkum ituvaraiyiṟ kiṭaitta entat taṇṇīriṉum mikac ciṟantatāy, iṉi varuṅkālamellām ēḻai makkaḷukku  uṟṟatoru naṭpeṉap payaṉpaṭuvatāyc caṭutiyil mēṟ kiḷampiṟṟu.
itō nampikkaiyaṟṟavarkaḷukkup peṉṉam periya tōr uṇmaiyāṉa ceyti! kaṭpulaṉākāta oru viṉaimutalāl veḷik koṇarappeṟṟu, ettakaiyōrāṉum etirmaṟukkak kūṭāta ōr uṇmai nikaḻcci! iyaṟkaipporuṇūl Natural Science. taṇṇīr kaṇṭeṭukka muṭiyāteṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu; āṉāl maṉanūlō `nī moḻintatu poy, ēṉeṉṟāl, inta maṇṇulakamāṉatu cuḻaṉṟucelluṅ kālamvaraiyum ciṟanta veḷḷamāyp puraṇṭōṭuñ ceḻumpuṉal uḷḷa iṭam ituveṉṟu nāṉ kuṟittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ eṉak kūṟiṟṟu. ki.pi. 1864ām āṇṭumutal; cikkākō nakarattil eṭukkappaṭṭa intat tuḷaikkiṇaṟṟiliruntu nāḷ oṉṟukku ēḻarai ilakkam marakkāl taṇṇīr viḻukkāṭu mēlōṭi varukiṉṟatu. mēlum, ittaṇṇīraik kuḷaṅkaḷiṉum mikap periya toṭṭikaḷiṉuṅ koṇṭu pōy nirappik kaṭṭivaittu, maḻai kālattil ciṟitēṟak kuṟaiya nāṉūṟṟeḻupattāṟu maṇaṅku paṉikkaṭṭi ceytu viṟṟuc ceṭṭup piṭikkiṉṟārkaḷ. iṉṉum ippaṉikkaṭṭiyai vēṇṭumpōtu nāṉku maṭaṅkākkiyum viṟkalām. evarum maṟuttaṟku iyalātu, amerikka makkaḷiṉ kaṇṇetirē nikaḻnta ivvuṇmai nikaḻcciyiṉāl, makkaḷiṉ mukattēyuḷḷa puṟakkaṇkaḷukkup pulaṉākāmal in nilapparappiṉ mēluṅ kīḻum neṭuntolaivil maṟaintu kiṭakkum evvakaip poruḷkaḷum avar tam akakkaṇṇiṉ etirē iṉitu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutalum, at tōṟṟat tiṉutaviyāl muyaṉṟu avaṟṟaip puṟakkaṇkaḷuṅ kāṇumpaṭi ceytu akkāṭciyiṉ uṇmaiyait teḷintu payaṉ peṟutalum uṇṭeṉpatu naṉku tuṇiyappaṭu kiṉṟataṉṟō?
iṉi, it teḷivukkāṭciyiṉ utaviyāṟ kāṇāmaṟ pōṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum kaṇṭeṭukkalām eṉpataṟku uṇmaiyāy nikaḻnta maṟṟoru nikaḻcciyai, ittuṟaiyil mikavallarāṉa maṟṟōr āciriyaratu nūliliruntu (How to thought - read by Dr. James Coates, Ph.D., F.A.S.) eṭuttu īṇṭu moḻipeyarttu varaikiṉṟām: - tiyaṭōr eṉṉum oru nīrāvik kappal piḷāṇṭu eṉṉum maṟṟoru kappal ilivarpūl eṉṉuṅ kaṭaṟṟuṟai paṭṭiṉattaic cērnta aṉṟaikkē vantu cērntatu. aḵtu eṅkaḷuṭaiya kappalaip pārkkilum periyatu; eṉṟālum ataṉ kaṇ uḷḷa carakkum eṅkaḷ kappalil uḷḷataip pōṉṟatē. `ellām cevvaiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu terivikkum poruṭṭāka nāṉ uṭaiyavarkaḷiṭam ceṉṟa aṉṟaikku, mīkāṉ mārṭṭaṉ eṉpavarai mikavuṅ kalavaramāṉa nilaimaiyil etirppaṭṭēṉ; ēṉeṉṟāl, tamatu campaḷamum tammōṭu vēlaipārppavar campaḷamuñ cērnta tokaikku īṭāṉa vilaiyuḷḷa muppatu pañcup potikaḷai avar iḻantu viṭṭār. avar ataṉporuṭṭu mikavuṅ kalaṅkiṉamaiyāl, avartam maṉaiviyum āṟṟāmai mikkavaḷākit, teḷivukkāṭciyil tiṟamaivāynta ōr iḷam peṇṇai yuṭaiyarāṉa haṭcaṉ eṉṉuṅ kappaṟṟalaivaṉiṭañ ceṉṟu ucāvumpaṭi avarait tūṇṭiṉāḷ. nāṅkaḷ iruvēmum teḷivukkāṭciyaip paṟṟiya ceytiyil aiyuṟavu koṇṭavarkaḷāka iruntōm. `ikkālattiya mantira mūṭṭaiyaip pōṉṟavaṟṟil tām talaiyiṭṭukkoḷḷa viruppam illaiyeṉṟu mārṭṭaṉ mutalil terivittār; āṉāl, muṭivākat tam maṉaiviyiṉ vēṇṭukōḷukku iṇaṅkippōka uṭaṉpaṭṭār.
mutalil mālumi haṭcaṉukku oṉṟēkāl rūpā kaṭṭaṇañ celuttiyapiṉ, teḷivuk kāṭciyuṭaiya avviḷam peṇṇaik kaṇṭupēca iṭampeṟṟōm. teḷivuk kāṭciyuṭaiya aṟituyilukku av viḷampeṇ celuttap paṭṭapiṉ, mārṭṭaṉ ap peṇṇiṉ valatu kaiyait tamatu valakkaiyiṟ piṭittuk koṭu, tām kēṭka virumpiyavaikaḷaik kēṭkalām eṉṟu kaṟpikkappaṭṭār.
avaḷ kūṟiya viṭaikaḷ piṉvarumāṟu iruntaṉa:  - tiyaṭōr eṉṉuṅ kappal mutaliṟ puṟappaṭṭa tuṟaimukattiṟkut tirumpa niṉaiviṉāṟ ceṉṟāḷ. ak kappalāṉatu ilivarppūlai viṭṭu aṭlāṇṭik kaṭalaik kuṟukkē kaṭantu, mēlintiya tīvukaḷiṉ ūṭē maṟupaṭiyum putu ārliyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattiṟkup pōṉavaḻiyait tirumpac collik kāṭṭiyatōṭu, taṉkaikaḷālun toṭarntu cuṭṭikkoṇṭu vantāḷ. kaṭaiciyāka, `ām, pañcup poti kāṇāmaṟpōṉatu ivviṭattilētāṉ. cuṟṟiluñ civappuk kuṟiyuḷḷa oru periya kaṟuppuk kappaliṉ mēṟṟaṭṭilē avaikaḷ vaikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟu avaḷ moḻintāḷ, ataṉ piṟaku, ak kappal taṉ iruppiṭattiṟkut tirumpi vanta vaḻiyait taṉ coṟkaḷālēyaṉṟik kaiyiṉāluṅ kuṟippiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvā ḷāṉāḷ; āṉāl, aṭlāṇṭik kaṭalait tirumpak kuṟukkiṭṭu vantapiṉ, ilivarppūlai nōkki airiṣ kālvāyil varuvataiviṭṭup pirañcuk karaiyai nōkkic celvatu pōl iṅkiliṣ kālvāyiṉ pakkamāyt tirumpiṉāḷ. ataṉpiṉ, taṉ kaiyai nīṭṭi `ō, itō pañcup potikaḷ; aṅkuḷḷavarkaḷ ā, evvaḷavu pakaṭiyāṉa makkaḷāyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ; avarkaḷ iṅkilīṣ pēcavillai. uṭaṉē, mārṭṭaṉ mīkāmaṉ `nallatu, atutāṉ tāma mīkāmaṉiṉ piraṉvik, eṉṟār. niyūārliyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattiṉ tuṟaimukattil tamatu kappalōṭu niṉṟu koṇṭirunta avvamerikkar kappal taṉakkuriya pañcuc carakkai ēṟṟik koṇṭiruntatu.
appōtu tiyaṭōr tāṉuñ carakkēṟṟikkoṇṭu pirañcut tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamākiya hāvarukkup pōvatāyiruntatu. iṅṅaṉam ap peṇmakaḷ taṉatu teḷivuk kāṭciyāl, aṟintu coṉṉavaṟṟaik kēṭṭu maṉa niṟaivu uṭaiyavarāy, mārṭṭaṉ mīkāmaṉ tamatu illattiṟkuc ceṉṟu, uṭaṉē tāma mīkāmaṉukku eḻutik kāṇāmaṟpōṉa tamatu carakkaip paṟṟik kēṭṭār. ciṟitu kālaṅkaḻittup, pañcuppoti, uṇmaiyākavē aṅkē uḷaveṉṟum, avai tuṟaimukattiṉiṉṟum picakāka eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum, avai evarkku uriyaṉavāy iruntālum viṟpaṉai ceytaṟku āyattamāyiruntaṉa veṉṟum avarkku viṭaivantatu. āṉāl, mārṭṭaṉ mīkāmaṉ, avai tirumpa aṉuppappaṭutaṟku ām celavu tokai yaṉuppiṉāl, appañcuppotikaḷ uṭaṉē avarkku aṉuppappaṭumeṉpatun terivikkappaṭṭatu. avar aṅṅaṉamē ceyya pirañcu nāṭṭu hāvar tuṟaimukattiliruntu iṅkilāntilirukkum ilivarppūl tuṟaimukattiṟku varutaṟkuriya celavu maṭṭum uṭaiyaṉavāy appañcup potikaḷ uriya kālattē avariṭam vantu cērntaṉa. ivai tām uṇmaiyāka nikaḻntavai; āṉāl, annikaḻcci yiṉiyalpai viḷakkutaṟku yāṉ pukavillai. eṉṉum immeyc ceytiyāl, paṉṉāṭkaḷukkumuṉ neṭuntolaiviṟ ṟavaṟip pōṉa poruḷkaḷum teḷivukkāṭci yuṭaiyārāl eḷitiṟ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭum uṇmai naṉku nilaipeṟukiṉṟataṉṟō?
iṉi, nōyāl varuntuvōriṉ nōyiṉ iyalpai aṟiyamāṭṭāmal tavikkum maruttuvar ataṉait tiṭṭamāy aṟintu koḷḷutaṟkun teḷivukkāṭci peritum utavi purivatākum. uṭampiṉ uḷḷē amainta karuvikaḷiṟṟōṉṟiya nōykaḷaip puṟakkaṇkaḷ maṭṭum uṭaiya maruttuvar yāṅṅaṉam uṇaravallār? akattuḷḷa nōyait tiṭṭamāka aṟiya māṭṭāmaiyāl, anta nōykkuttakka maruntaik koṭukka ēlāmal, takāta maruntaikkoṭuttup piḻaipaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. nōyāḷikaḷum poruṭcelavu ceytum maruntuṇṭum payaṉ peṟāmal neṭukat tuṉpuṟṟu uyiraiyun tuṟakkiṉṟaṉar. āṅkila maruttuvariṟ palar nōyāḷikaḷiṉ akattē nōyirukku miṭanteriyāmal nōyillā vēṟiṭaṅkaḷai aṟuttup pārttu ēmāṟṟam aṭaikiṉṟaṉar.
avartam aṟiyāmaiyāl, annōyāḷikaḷum āṟṟutaṟkariya tuṉpameytic cākiṉṟaṉar. ātalāl, maruttuvap payiṟciyōṭu, teḷivuk kāṭciyāl nōyiṉ taṉmaiyaik kaṇṭaṟiyum muṟaikaḷilum paḻakit tērvarāyiṉ attakaiya maruttuvarāl ulakiṟku viḷaiyum naṉmaikaḷ aḷaviṭappaṭā. nōyiṉ iyalpu teḷiyappaṭāmaiyāl oru nōyāḷiyum avarukku maruttuvañ ceyta āṅkila maruttuvarun teḷivuk kāṭciyiṉ utaviyāl attuṉpaṅkaḷ nīṅkappeṟṟamaikku uṇmaiyāy nikaḻnta maṟṟoru varalāṟṟiṉai mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa aṟituyiṟ pulamai āciriyar (Dr. James Coates How to thought - Read) nūlilirunteṭuttu moḻipeyart teḻutukiṉṟām: - `iyaṟkaiyākavē teḷivukkāṭci eṉṉum oru perum pēṟṟiṉai aṭaintu taṉ maṭṭil uyir vāḻum oru perumāṭṭikku aṟimukamākum nalviṉaiyai āciriyar uvilṭ eṉpavar peṟṟār. tamakku naṇpaṉāyuḷḷavaṉ oruvaṉ pala āṇṭukaḷāka ovvōr iravum pala nāḻikai nēram varaiyil mutukilum mārpilum koṭiyatoru nōyuṟṟu vantaṉaṉeṉṟum, nāṭcellac cellap piṟaku avaṉ iravuḻuḻutum oru nāṟkāliyil uṭkārntirukka vēṇṭiyavaṉ āyiṉā ṉeṉṟum, ataṉāl avaṉ kālkaḷ vīṅkat tuvaṅkiṉa veṉṟum avvāciriyar apperumāṭṭikkuc colliṉar.
inta nōyāḷi ceṉṟa mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷāka ilaṇṭaṉmā nakarattiluḷḷa palarāl muṟaiyāka maruttuvañ ceyyappaṭṭu vantaṉar. neñcappaiyil iṉṉateṉṟu viḷaṅkāta ētō oru picaku irukkavēṇṭumeṉṟu cilar coṉṉārkaḷ; vēṟucilar atu narampunōy eṉṟu kūṟiṉar; oruvar ataṉaip poruttup piṭippu eṉṟār; kaṭaiciyāka oruvar mutukelumpu mikavum aḻukip pōyiṟṟu eṉṟār.
uvilṭ eṉpavariṉ nēyar, muṉṉatākac ceytu koṇṭa ēṟpāṭṭiṉpaṭi avarai vantu kaṇṭu, mēṟcolliya perumāṭṭiyāraiyum pārttār. inta ammaiyār cummā avarai uṟṟu nōkkiṉār. ataṉ piṉ, avar avvaṟaiyai viṭṭuc ceṉṟapiṉ, anta ammaiyār am maruttuvarai nōkkip piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiṉār: aḵtu ettakaiya nōy eṉpatai yāṉ kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ. avaruṭampu paḷiṅkupōṟ ṟeḷivāy iruntateṉṉa yāṉ ataṉai avvaḷavu viḷakkamāyk kaṇṭēṉ. avaratu neñcappaikkuppiṉṉē kaṭukkāy aḷaviṉatākiya oru kaṭṭi puṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu; aḵtu aḻukku niṟamuṭaiyatā yirukkiṉṟatu; atu uṭaintu pōkumpōl āṭukiṉṟatu. muṟṟilum ōyvāyiruppatait tavira vēṟetuvum avarkku naṉmai ceyyātu.
ataṉmēl uvilṭ eṉpavar kūṟiyatāvatu: `avvammaiyār karutic coṉṉatu ituveṉa yāṉ uṭaṉē kaṇṭēṉ. ākavē ataṉaip piṉvarumāṟu eṉ naṇpariṉ maruttuvarukku eḻuta uṭkārntēṉ: eṉ naṇparukkuḷḷa nōyiṉ taṉmai iṉṉateṉṟu kaṇṭu koṇṭēṉeṉa nampukiṉṟēṉ iṟakkattil uḷḷa cennīrppaiyil avarkku oru vīkkam oru kaṭukkāyaḷavu irukkiṉṟatu. itutāṉ neñcappaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa ciṟitu iṭappeyarcciyai uṇṭākkiyatākum; ikkaṭṭiyāṉatu vilāvelumpu kaḷiṉ iṭaiyiluḷḷa narampukaḷai nerukkutalāl mutukiṟ perunōyum mārpiṉ muṉpuṟattuḷḷa tōliṉkaṇ nōyum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. nāḷaikku nīṅkaḷ pārkkaccellum maruttuvarukku, yāmaṟinta innōyiṉ nilaiyait terivittu, avar colvatu iṉṉateṉṟu eṉakku aṟiviyuṅkaḷ.
aṅṅaṉamē annōyāḷi anta maruttuvariṭan terivittatil apperumāṭṭi annōyaippaṟṟik kūṟiyatu tāṉ uṇmaiyeṉa uṟutip paṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉōṭu annōyai nīkkutaṟkuc ceyya vēṇṭuvatum muṟṟum ōyntiruttalēyā meṉpatum maruttuvar ellārāṉum oppukkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. avvāṟē annōyāḷi ōyntirukka aḵtu avaraiviṭṭu akaṉṟatu. ivvuṇmai varalāṟṟiṉāl, nōyiṉ iyalpai uḷḷavāṟu uṇarntu, ataṉai nīkkutaṟkuc ceyyum muṟaiyun tiṭṭamākat teḷintu koḷḷutaṟkut teḷivukkāṭci ettuṇai yutavi ceykiṉṟa teṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟataṉṟō?
18. cila eccarippukaḷ
mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavāṟu aṟituyilaip piṟarpāl varuvittu, ataṉutaviyāṟ pala uyarnta naṉmaikaḷai viḷaivikka vēṇṭupavarkaḷ, itaṉaip payiṟci ceykiṉṟa pōtu kaṭṭāyamāyk karuttiṟpatittu naṭakkavēṇṭiya kuṟippukaḷ cila uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ, mutalāvatu, oruvarai aṟituyiliṟ celuttukiṉṟapōtu tuvakkattilēyē piṉvarumāṟu colli avarait tamatu kaṭṭaḷaikku uṭaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷakkaṭavar: ippōtu ummai aṟituyilukkuc celuttap pōkiṉṟēṉ. ummai aṟituyiliṟ celuttuvatu umakkum eṉakkum ulakattiṟkum naṉmaiyai uṇṭākkutaṟ poruṭṭēyām. ātalāl, eṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi nīr aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟu, attuyilil irukkaiyil, yāṉ collukiṟapaṭiyē ceytu, tirumpa yāṉ ataṉiṉiṉṟum ummai viḻikkac collukaiyil eṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭiyē uṭaṉē viḻittu eḻakkaṭavīr. eṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi yellām uḷḷaṭaṅki naṭakkakkaṭavīr. eṉṟu valiyuṟuttic colli, ataṉpiṟaku avar aṟituyiliṟ celkaiyilum iṭaiyiṭaiyē mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu kaṭṭuṟuttic colluka.
iraṇṭāvatu: avar aṟituyilil irukkaiyil, naṉmaiyāṉa coṟkaḷaiyē teḷivāy valiyuṟuttic colluka. maṟantun tīmaiyāṉa coṟkaḷaic collaṟka. umatu uṭampu mikavum naṉṟāy irukkiṉṟatu. umatu aṟivu mikavum teḷivāyirukkiṉṟatu. umatu mūḷaiyum nuraiyīral neñcappai tīṉippai malakkuṭar mutalāṉa akakkaruvikaḷum mikavuñ cevvaiyāṉa nilaiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. nīr eppōtum nalla niṉaivum nalla eṇṇamum uṭaiyavarāyiruppīr. nīr eṉṟum nalla ceykaikaḷaiyē ceyvīr eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttic colluka. avar attūkkattilirukkaiyil nōyaippaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ ētēṉun tappittavaṟik kūṭavarāmal viḻippāyp pēcuka.
mūṉṟāvatu: aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟavar ataṉiṉiṉṟum eḻuntapiṉ iṉṉiṉṉa nāḷil iṉṉiṉṉa nērattil iṉṉiṉṉavaṟṟaic ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟu avar attuyilil irukkaiyil valiyuṟuttic coṉṉāl, avar aṅṅaṉamē tām viḻittiruntāluṅ kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṟ kuṟippiṭṭapaṭiyēceyvar.ākaiyāṟ, piṉ naṭakka vēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic colli avarukkuk kaṟpikkumpōtu, cevvaiyāy ārāyntu collat takkavaṟṟait tiruttamākac collal vēṇṭum. oruvarait tuyiṟṟukiṉṟa maṟṟoruvar tammaittavira vēṟevarum avarait tuyiṟṟutal ākātu eṉṉum eṇṇam uṭaiya varāyiruntāl, av eṇṇattai aṟituyilil iruppavarkkut tiṭṭamāyk kūṟuka. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, cilamuṟai aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭṭa oruvar piṟaku evarāṉum eḷitilē aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭutaṟku icaintavar āvar. inta uḷavait terinta tīyōr evarēṉum ivvaṟituyiṉ muṟaiyait terintukoṇṭu avarukku eḷitilē aṟituyilai varuvittu avarait tam vaḻippaṭuttikkoṇṭu avaraip palavakaiyilum ēmāṟṟavuṅkūṭum. ātalāl, nallōrāṉa tuyiṟṟuvōr tammāl tuyiṟṟappaṭuvōr piṟar evarāṉum iṅṅaṉan tuyiṟṟappaṭutal ākātu eṉṟu kaṭṭuraittuc colliviṭṭāṟ, piṉpu immuṟaiyiṟ paḻakiya maṟṟevarum avarait tuyilacceytal iyalātu. iṉṉum avar aṟituyilil irukkaiyil avarukkut tām kaṭṭuṟuttic colliyavaikaḷai avar attuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuntapiṉ niṉaittalākātu eṉṟu collavēṇṭiṉāl, aṅṅaṉamē colluka; avar viḻittapiṉ attūkkattil nikaḻntavai kaḷai oru ciṟitum uṇarār.
nāṉkāvatu: mūvakaippaṭṭa aṟituyiliṟ celuttap paṭṭavarkaḷum annilaiyilirukkuṅkāl, avarkaḷukkut tōṉṟumāṟu viḷaivitta uruveḷit tōṟṟaṅkaḷai maṟaiyumāṟuceytu, avaratu uṇarvu eppōtumpōla oḻuṅkāṉa nilaiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa teṉavuṅ kaṟpittu, avarai viḻikkac ceyvataṟku maṟantuviṭalākātu.
aintāvatu: tuyiṟṟappaṭṭavar aṟituyilil irukkum pōtu avariṭam ētēṉuṅ kēṭkavirumpiṉāl `nīr ippōtu cevvaiyāka irukkiṉṟīrā? nīr ētāvatu immuṟaikku eṉakku aṟivikkakkūṭumā? eṉṟu kēṭka vēṇṭumē yallāl `nīr cevvaiyā yirukkiṉṟīr eṉṟu niṉaikkiṉṟēṉ, yāṉ ceyyavēṇṭiyamuṟai iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiviyum eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭuk kēṭṭalākātu. ēṉeṉil, aṟituyilil irukkaiyil tuyiṟṟuvōr ētu kaṭṭaḷai yiṭukiṉṟārō akkaṭṭaḷaippaṭiyē tuyilvōr ceyvar; kaṭṭaḷai yiṭāmaṟ kēṭṭāl avaratu uḷḷattil appōtu iyaṟkaiyākat tōṉṟuvaṉavaṟṟai uraippar. avaṟṟaik kēṭṭu uṇmai yiṉṉa teṉṟu uṇarntu ataṟkēṟpac ceyvaṉavaṟṟaic ceyyalām.
āṟāvatu: mūvakaiyāṉa aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭ ṭōraiyum attuyiliṉiṉṟum eḻuppa vēṇṭuṅ kālattil eḻuppiviṭutaṟkuc ciṟitum maṟantu pōkalākātu. oruvarukku varum aṟituyilāṉatu iṟaivaṉatu tiruvaruḷiṉpattil nikaḻvatātalāl, avar avviṉpat tuyiliṉiṉṟuñ caṭutiyil viḻitteḻa viruppam illātavarāy iruppar. atupaṟṟi maṉantaṭumāṟi uḷḷaṅ kalaṅkal vēṇṭām. nīvir vēṇṭum pōtu viḻitteḻa avarukku viruppam illaiyāṉāl `evvaḷavu nēran tuyila virumpukiṉṟīr? eṉṟu avaraiyē viṉāvuka. iraṇṭumaṇi nēram mūṉṟumaṇi nēran tūṅkal vēṇṭumeṉa virumpuvarāyiṉ aṅṅaṉamē avarait tūṅkaviṭuka. iṉṉum pala maṇinēran tūṅkal vēṇṭum eṉparāyiṉ, ataṟku icaiya iyalāviṭṭāl `avvaḷavu nēraṅ kūṭātu, iṉṉuṅ kuṟaiyac collum, iṉṉuṅ kuṟaiyac collum eṉṟu colli avarai varavara iṇakkik koṇṭuvantu arai, allatu oṉṟu, allatu oṉṟarai, allatu iraṇṭu maṇi nēram varaiyil ēṟkumāṟu tūṅkaviṭṭu, ataṉpiṉ iraṇṭu kaikaḷaiyum nīṭṭi, uḷḷaṅkaikaḷaiyum viralkaḷaiyum mēl nōkkit tirumpik, kaṭaic ciṟuviral tavira, ēṉaiviralkaḷaic cērttapaṭiyāy avaratu aṭiyilirun tāyiṉum muḻaṅkāliliruntāyiṉum mēlnōkki muṭivaraiyil toṭṭēṉun toṭāmalēṉum palakāl taṭavi, avartam neṟṟimēluṅ kaṇṇiṟaippaikaḷiṉ mēlum vāyiṉāl meṉkāṟṟuṇṭāka ūti `viḻittuk koḷka eṉṟāl viḻittukkoḷvar. aṅṅaṉam viḻikkaiyiṟ kaṇṇiṟaippai kaḷait tiṟakka māṭṭāmaṟ ciṟitu varuntu varāyiṉ, avarai iraṇṭoru nimiṭam cummā irukkaviṭṭu ataṉpiṉ `kaṇṇait tiṟantupārum eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāl avar viḻittukkoḷvar. allatu, yāṉ irupatu eṇṇiyavuṭaṉ viḻittukkoḷvīr eṉṟu collik kaikaḷai mēṉōkkit taṭavukaiyil `oṉṟu - iraṇṭu - mūṉṟu eṉṟu āḻnta kuraliṟ coṟlik koṇṭē ceṉṟu irupatu muṭintatum `ippōtu nīr viḻittukkoḷḷak kaṭavīr eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭuk kaiyait taṭṭiṉāl uṭaṉē viḻittuk koḷvar.eṉṟu itukāṟum yōkanittirai eṉṉum aṟituyilākiya aruṅkalaittiṟam iṉitu viḷakkappaṭṭatu. ivvarumaruntiṉum uyarnta kalaiyaip paḻaki ulakattavar nalam peṟuka!
